4. 關鍵 SQL 範例

以下 $n 為 sqlx 綁定參數。所有交易使用預設 READ COMMITTED 即可（依賴條件式 UPDATE 的 row lock）。已在上述容器實測 4.1–4.7、4.12（恢復權杖：一次性、過期、換發）、4.13（退訂）、4.14（新帳號每日上限）、4.15（解凍與欠款結案）、4.3s（拒付：凍結、扣回 topup 批次、不足記 wallet_debts、重送冪等）、4.9（帳號與訪客去識別化）、4.10（認領通知：第一筆即時、其後每日彙整累加）、4.11（下架與檢舉連動）（含 4.3 的批次扣點順序、退回原批次與過期 bonus 作廢、併發超額、餘額不足、達標觸發、差額分配加總等於差額、NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE、禮物卡重複兌換、星星扣回不為負、Σ lots = balance 對帳）。4.3r 為企業批次匯出與補發。4.3c、4.3d、4.3g 中「接 4.3R」處為示意拼接，實際為同一條語句。

-- ================================================================
-- 4.1 防超額認領：同一交易內「條件式 UPDATE + INSERT」
--     $1=item_id  $2=qty  $3=guest_id  $4=claimer_name  $5=note
-- ================================================================
BEGIN;

-- (a) 冪等：先佔 key；0 rows 表示已存在 → 比對 request_hash，相同則重放 response_body，不同則 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT
INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (key, scope, request_hash)
VALUES ($6, $7, $8)
ON CONFLICT (scope, key) DO NOTHING
RETURNING key;

-- (b) 條件式 UPDATE：取得品項 row lock，同時驗證清單狀態與剩餘量
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET qty_claimed = i.qty_claimed + $2
  FROM wishlists w
 WHERE i.id = $1
   AND w.id = i.wishlist_id
   AND w.status = 'active' AND w.deleted_at IS NULL
   AND i.deleted_at IS NULL
   AND i.funding_mode = 'quantity'
   AND i.qty_claimed + $2 <= i.qty_needed
RETURNING i.id, i.wishlist_id, i.qty_needed, i.qty_claimed, w.claim_ttl_hours;
-- 0 rows → ROLLBACK，再用下方 (d) 判斷是 404 / WISHLIST_CLOSED / FUNDING_MODE_MISMATCH / ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED

-- (c) 寫入 claim（與 (b) 同一交易，任何失敗都會一起 rollback）
-- $10 = now() + claim_ttl_hours（claim_ttl_hours 為 NULL 則傳 NULL）
-- 同一 guest 已有有效認領時，違反 claims_item_guest_active_uk（SQLSTATE 23505）→ rollback，回 409 CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS
INSERT INTO claims (item_id, guest_id, claimer_name, qty, note, expires_at)
VALUES ($1, $3, $4, $2, $5, $10)
RETURNING id, status, created_at;

-- (e) 交易尾端：存下回應供 24h 內重放
UPDATE idempotency_keys SET response_status = 201, response_body = $9::jsonb
 WHERE scope = $7 AND key = $6;

COMMIT;

-- (d) 失敗歸因（在 rollback 後以獨立查詢執行）
SELECT i.deleted_at IS NOT NULL OR w.deleted_at IS NOT NULL AS gone,
       w.status, i.funding_mode, i.qty_needed - i.qty_claimed AS remaining
  FROM wishlist_items i JOIN wishlists w ON w.id = i.wishlist_id
 WHERE i.id = $1;
-- 無 row 或 gone → 404 NOT_FOUND；status <> 'active' → 409 WISHLIST_CLOSED；
-- funding_mode = 'crowdfund' → 409 FUNDING_MODE_MISMATCH；否則 409 ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED（附 remaining）

-- 併發保證：兩個交易同時對同一 item 執行 (b)，後者會在 row lock 上等待，
-- 前者 COMMIT 後以最新 qty_claimed 重新評估 WHERE（READ COMMITTED 的 EvalPlanQual），超額者 0 rows。
-- items_qty_claimed_range CHECK 為最後防線。

-- ================================================================
-- 4.2 修改數量 / 取消（回補）
-- ================================================================
-- 修改數量：以差額 delta = new_qty - old_qty 做條件式 UPDATE（delta 可為負）
BEGIN;
SELECT qty FROM claims WHERE id = $1 AND status IN ('reserved','purchased') FOR UPDATE;  -- 取得 old_qty 並鎖 claim
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET qty_claimed = i.qty_claimed + $2            -- $2 = delta
 WHERE i.id = $3 AND i.qty_claimed + $2 <= i.qty_needed
RETURNING i.qty_claimed;                            -- 0 rows → ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED
UPDATE claims SET qty = qty + $2 WHERE id = $1;
COMMIT;

-- 取消回補：單一語句完成（狀態守衛避免重複回補）
WITH c AS (
  UPDATE claims
     SET status = 'cancelled', cancelled_at = now()
   WHERE id = $1 AND status IN ('reserved','purchased')
  RETURNING item_id, qty
)
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET qty_claimed = i.qty_claimed - c.qty
  FROM c
 WHERE i.id = c.item_id
RETURNING i.id, i.qty_needed, i.qty_claimed;
-- 0 rows 表示已 cancelled / delivered / 不存在 → 依 claim 現況回 204（已取消）或 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION
-- 逾期釋出（/internal/jobs/tick 每分鐘執行；D7）：批次轉 expired 並依品項回補
WITH c AS (
  UPDATE claims SET status = 'expired'
   WHERE status = 'reserved' AND expires_at IS NOT NULL AND expires_at <= now()
  RETURNING item_id, qty
), s AS (SELECT item_id, sum(qty) AS qty FROM c GROUP BY item_id)
UPDATE wishlist_items i SET qty_claimed = i.qty_claimed - s.qty
  FROM s WHERE i.id = s.item_id;
-- 轉 purchased 時一併 SET expires_at = NULL。

-- ================================================================
-- 4.3 點數錢包與眾籌（P2-A）、點數批次（D23）、禮物卡（P2-B）、回饋星星（P2-B）
--     鎖順序全文件統一（D22-12）：wishlists（FOR SHARE）→ point_wallets（FOR UPDATE）→ wishlist_items（條件式 UPDATE）→ contributions；
--     reward_accounts 排在 wallet 之後（兌換：wallet → reward_accounts）；背景 job 一律先鎖 wallet（依 id 排序）、最後才動 item。
--     所有批次異動都先持有 wallet 列鎖，因此 point_lots 不需另行加鎖。
-- ================================================================
-- 4.3a 儲值入帳（webhook 驗簽通過後；provider_txn_id 冪等）→ 建立 topup 批次
BEGIN;
UPDATE topups
   SET status = 'paid', paid_at = now()
 WHERE provider = $1 AND provider_txn_id = $2 AND status = 'pending' AND amount = $3
RETURNING id, wallet_id, amount;          -- 0 rows → 已處理過（回 200 不重複入帳）或金額不符（記 audit 並告警）
UPDATE point_wallets SET balance = balance + $3 WHERE id = $4 RETURNING balance;     -- $4 = wallet_id；這裡取得 wallet 列鎖
WITH lot AS (                                                                         -- 建立 topup 批次：可退現金、不到期
  INSERT INTO point_lots (wallet_id, source, source_ref_id, original_points, remaining_points, cash_refundable)
  VALUES ($4, 'topup', $5, $3, $3, true) RETURNING id                                 -- $5 = topups.id
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $4, id, $3, $6, 'topup', 'topup', $5 FROM lot;                                 -- $6 = 上一句 RETURNING 的 balance
COMMIT;

-- 4.3b 認捐交易（POST /items/{item_id}/contributions）：$1=item_id $2=points $3=user_id $4=message $5=is_anonymous
BEGIN;
-- 冪等 key 佔用（同 4.1 (a)）略

-- ⓪ 先鎖清單（共享鎖，擋住清單同時被封存 / 關閉）；$6 = 由 item 無鎖讀得的 wishlist_id
SELECT id FROM wishlists WHERE id = $6 AND status = 'active' AND deleted_at IS NULL FOR SHARE;   -- 0 rows → 404 / WISHLIST_CLOSED

-- ① 鎖 wallet 並扣點；0 rows → 查 wallet：status = 'frozen' → 403 WALLET_FROZEN；否則餘額不足 → 409 INSUFFICIENT_POINTS
UPDATE point_wallets SET balance = balance - $2
 WHERE user_id = $3 AND balance >= $2 AND status = 'active'      -- 凍結的錢包（拒付 / 風控）不可認捐
RETURNING id, balance;

-- ② 條件式 UPDATE 品項：硬上限、須 open 且未過截止；達標當下同句改 funded
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET pledged_points = i.pledged_points + $2,
       funding_status = CASE WHEN i.pledged_points + $2 = i.target_points
                             THEN 'funded'::funding_status ELSE i.funding_status END
 WHERE i.id = $1 AND i.deleted_at IS NULL AND i.funding_mode = 'crowdfund'
   AND i.funding_status = 'open' AND i.funding_deadline > now()
   AND i.pledged_points + $2 <= i.target_points
RETURNING i.wishlist_id, i.target_points, i.pledged_points, i.funding_status,
          i.fulfillment_type, i.catalog_product_id;
-- 0 rows → ROLLBACK（①的扣點一併還原），再歸因：
--   funding_status IN ('funded','fulfilled') → 409 ITEM_FUNDED
--   funding_status = 'open' 且 pledged_points + $2 > target_points → 409 CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED（remaining_points = target - pledged）
--   expired / 已過截止 → 409 FUNDING_EXPIRED；清單 closed / archived → 409 WISHLIST_CLOSED；funding_mode = 'quantity' → 409 FUNDING_MODE_MISMATCH；其餘 404

-- ③ 認捐紀錄
INSERT INTO contributions (item_id, wishlist_id, user_id, wallet_id, points, message, is_anonymous)
VALUES ($1, $6, $3, $7, $2, $4, $5) RETURNING id;                       -- $6=wishlist_id $7=wallet_id（來自 ①②）$9=回傳的 id

-- ④ 依序扣批次：reward_bonus（最早到期優先）→ gift_card → topup；同時寫 allocations 與 ledger
--    $7 = wallet_id  $9 = 剛建立的 contribution id  $2 = points  $8 = ① 扣點後的 wallet.balance
WITH cand AS (
  SELECT l.id, l.remaining_points AS rem,
         sum(l.remaining_points) OVER o AS cum,
         row_number() OVER o AS ord
    FROM point_lots l
   WHERE l.wallet_id = $7 AND l.remaining_points > 0
     AND (l.expires_at IS NULL OR l.expires_at > now())              -- D31-7：即使到期 job 尚未執行，已過期批次也不可扣
  WINDOW o AS (ORDER BY CASE l.source WHEN 'reward_bonus' THEN 0 WHEN 'gift_card' THEN 1 WHEN 'topup' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END, l.expires_at NULLS LAST, l.created_at, l.id)
), take AS (
  SELECT id, ord, LEAST(rem, $2 - (cum - rem)) AS pts FROM cand WHERE cum - rem < $2
), upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = l.remaining_points - t.pts FROM take t WHERE l.id = t.id RETURNING l.id
), alloc AS (
  INSERT INTO contribution_lot_allocations (contribution_id, lot_id, points)
  SELECT $9, id, pts FROM take RETURNING 1
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $7, id, -pts,
       $8 + COALESCE(sum(pts) OVER (ORDER BY ord ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING), 0),
       'pledge', 'contribution', $9
  FROM take ORDER BY ord;
-- 之後檢查 Σ allocations.points = $p；不足（例如 bonus 批次剛過期、job 尚未清）→ ROLLBACK → 409 INSUFFICIENT_POINTS
SELECT COALESCE(sum(points), 0) AS allocated FROM contribution_lot_allocations WHERE contribution_id = $9;

-- ⑤ 僅當 ② 回傳 funding_status = 'funded'（pledged_points = target_points）：同交易內完成「捕獲 + 建採購單 + outbox」
UPDATE contributions SET status = 'captured', captured_at = now()
 WHERE item_id = $1 AND status = 'pledged';
INSERT INTO purchase_orders (item_id, fulfillment_type, merchant_id, amount, shipping_address_snapshot)
VALUES ($1, $10, $11, $12, $13)                                         -- $12=target_points；$13=應用層重新 AEAD 加密的收件快照
ON CONFLICT (item_id) DO UPDATE                                          -- 失敗重試重用同一列
   SET status = 'pending', failure_reason = NULL, amount = EXCLUDED.amount,
       shipping_address_snapshot = EXCLUDED.shipping_address_snapshot;
INSERT INTO outbox_events (kind, payload) VALUES ('order.place', jsonb_build_object('item_id', $1)), ('rewards.earn', jsonb_build_object('item_id', $1));   -- 星星改由 job 非同步賺取（見 4.3n），不在認捐交易內
COMMIT;
-- 認捐成功後（無論是否達標）：餘額低於自動儲值門檻 → 寫 outbox（見 4.3p），認捐交易不等待扣卡；餘額不足時仍直接回 INSUFFICIENT_POINTS。
-- 併發：兩人同時認捐剩餘不足的額度，後到者在 ② 的 row lock 上等待，前者 COMMIT 後條件不成立 → 0 rows → 後者整筆 rollback（含扣點）。
-- items_pledged_le_target 與 point_wallets_balance_nonneg 兩個 CHECK 為最後防線（對應 CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED / INSUFFICIENT_POINTS）。


-- 4.3R 退回原批次（共用語句；4.3c / 4.3d / 4.3f / 4.3g 都用它）
--   * 呼叫前必須已依序鎖定相關 wallet（獨立的 SELECT ... FOR UPDATE，使本語句取得鎖之後的最新快照），且 rel 內須帶狀態守衛，避免重複退點。
--   * 退回順序與扣點順序相反（topup → gift_card → reward_bonus）；若原 reward_bonus 批次已過期，該部分作廢：寫 release 後立即以 expire 抵銷，wallet 不增加。
--   * 每個批次一筆 ledger（含 lot_id）；balance_after 以視窗函數還原逐筆餘額。
--   * 部分退點（差額退回）的退回順序（D31-6）：topup → gift_card → reward_bonus（逆序，優先回補可退現金的批次）；整筆 released 則按 allocations 全數退回原批次。
-- 使用方式：WITH rel(contribution_id, wallet_id, back, ref_type, ref_id) AS ( ... )  接下列內容
-- 輸入 rel(contribution_id, wallet_id, back, ref_type, ref_id)：每筆認捐要退回的點數
-- 逆序退回：先 topup、再 gift_card、最後 reward_bonus；若原 reward_bonus 批次已過期 → 該部分作廢（release 後立即 expire）
, lkw AS (                                                       -- 先鎖 wallet（依 id 排序）
  SELECT w.id, w.balance FROM point_wallets w
   WHERE w.id IN (SELECT wallet_id FROM rel) ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE
), alloc AS (
  SELECT r.contribution_id, r.wallet_id, r.back, r.ref_type, r.ref_id, a.lot_id, l.source, l.expires_at,
         a.points - a.refunded_points AS avail,
         sum(a.points - a.refunded_points) OVER o AS cum,
         row_number() OVER o AS ord
    FROM rel r
    JOIN lkw ON lkw.id = r.wallet_id
    JOIN contribution_lot_allocations a ON a.contribution_id = r.contribution_id
    JOIN point_lots l ON l.id = a.lot_id
  WINDOW o AS (PARTITION BY r.contribution_id
               ORDER BY CASE l.source WHEN 'topup' THEN 0 WHEN 'gift_card' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, l.id)
), take AS (
  SELECT *, LEAST(avail, back - (cum - avail)) AS pts,
         (source = 'reward_bonus' AND expires_at <= now()) AS voided
    FROM alloc WHERE avail > 0 AND cum - avail < back
), a_upd AS (
  UPDATE contribution_lot_allocations a SET refunded_points = a.refunded_points + t.pts
    FROM take t WHERE a.contribution_id = t.contribution_id AND a.lot_id = t.lot_id RETURNING 1
), l_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = l.remaining_points + s.pts
    FROM (SELECT lot_id, sum(pts) AS pts FROM take WHERE NOT voided GROUP BY lot_id) s
   WHERE l.id = s.lot_id RETURNING 1
), w_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_wallets pw SET balance = pw.balance + s.pts
    FROM (SELECT wallet_id, sum(pts) AS pts FROM take WHERE NOT voided GROUP BY wallet_id) s
   WHERE pw.id = s.wallet_id RETURNING 1
), ent AS (                                                      -- 每個批次一筆 release；作廢者再補一筆 expire 抵銷
  SELECT wallet_id, lot_id, pts AS delta, 'release'::ledger_entry_type AS entry_type, ref_type, ref_id, contribution_id, ord * 2 AS k FROM take
  UNION ALL
  SELECT wallet_id, lot_id, -pts, 'expire', ref_type, ref_id, contribution_id, ord * 2 + 1 FROM take WHERE voided
), fin AS (                                                      -- 每個 wallet 異動後的餘額
  SELECT lkw.id AS wallet_id,
         lkw.balance + COALESCE((SELECT sum(pts) FROM take t WHERE t.wallet_id = lkw.id AND NOT t.voided), 0) AS balance_after
    FROM lkw
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT e.wallet_id, e.lot_id, e.delta,
       f.balance_after - COALESCE(sum(e.delta) OVER (PARTITION BY e.wallet_id ORDER BY e.contribution_id, e.k
                                                      ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING), 0),
       e.entry_type, e.ref_type, e.ref_id
  FROM ent e JOIN fin f USING (wallet_id)
 ORDER BY e.wallet_id, e.contribution_id, e.k;

-- 4.3c 撤回（DELETE /contributions/{id}，D22-1）：品項 open，或 expired 且在 7 天選擇期內（now < expired_at + 7d），
--      且該筆為 pledged → released，退回原批次；其餘（captured、選擇期已過、funded）→ 409 CONTRIBUTION_LOCKED
BEGIN;
SELECT wishlist_id, wallet_id, item_id, points - refunded_points AS back FROM contributions
 WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2 AND status = 'pledged';                  -- 無鎖讀取；之後 $3=wishlist_id $4=wallet_id $5=item_id $6=back
SELECT id FROM wishlists WHERE id = $3 FOR SHARE;                        -- wishlist
SELECT id FROM point_wallets WHERE id = $4 FOR UPDATE;                   -- wallet
UPDATE wishlist_items SET pledged_points = pledged_points - $6           -- item
 WHERE id = $5
   AND (funding_status = 'open'
        OR (funding_status = 'expired' AND expired_at + interval '7 days' > now()))   -- 其餘情況 0 rows → CONTRIBUTION_LOCKED
RETURNING pledged_points;
WITH rel(contribution_id, wallet_id, back, ref_type, ref_id) AS (
  SELECT id, wallet_id, points - refunded_points, 'contribution', id FROM contributions WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'pledged'
)
-- ↓ 接 4.3R 的語句主體（, lkw AS ( ... ) INSERT INTO point_ledger ...）
;
UPDATE contributions SET status = 'released', released_at = now(), refunded_points = points WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'pledged';   -- contribution
COMMIT;

-- 4.3d 截止（tick job）：open 且過截止 → expired；之後 7 天為捐贈者選擇期
UPDATE wishlist_items SET funding_status = 'expired', expired_at = now()
 WHERE funding_mode = 'crowdfund' AND funding_status = 'open' AND funding_deadline <= now()
RETURNING id, wishlist_id;                                               -- 對每個 id 寫 outbox 通知捐贈者

-- 選擇期結束仍 pledged 者自動退回原批次
BEGIN;
SELECT w.id FROM point_wallets w                                         -- 先鎖 wallet（依 id 排序）
 WHERE w.id IN (SELECT c.wallet_id FROM contributions c JOIN wishlist_items i ON i.id = c.item_id
                 WHERE c.status = 'pledged' AND i.funding_status = 'expired' AND i.expired_at + interval '7 days' <= now())
 ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE;
UPDATE wishlist_items SET pledged_points = 0                             -- expired 品項沒有 captured，pledged 全數退回後歸 0
 WHERE funding_status = 'expired' AND expired_at + interval '7 days' <= now();
WITH rel(contribution_id, wallet_id, back, ref_type, ref_id) AS (
  SELECT c.id, c.wallet_id, c.points - c.refunded_points, 'contribution', c.id
    FROM contributions c JOIN wishlist_items i ON i.id = c.item_id
   WHERE c.status = 'pledged' AND i.funding_status = 'expired' AND i.expired_at + interval '7 days' <= now()
)
-- ↓ 接 4.3R 的語句主體
;
UPDATE contributions c SET status = 'released', released_at = now(), refunded_points = c.points
  FROM wishlist_items i WHERE i.id = c.item_id AND c.status = 'pledged'
   AND i.funding_status = 'expired' AND i.expired_at + interval '7 days' <= now();
COMMIT;

-- 4.3e 轉投（POST /contributions/{id}/reallocate）：來源須為 expired 且在選擇期內；兩品項依 id 排序鎖定
BEGIN;
SELECT id FROM wishlists WHERE id = $6 FOR SHARE;                          -- $6 = 同清單的 wishlist_id
SELECT id FROM wishlist_items WHERE id IN ($1, $2) ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE;   -- $1=來源 item $2=目標 item（依 id 排序）
UPDATE wishlist_items t
   SET pledged_points = t.pledged_points + $3,                               -- $3=points
       funding_status = CASE WHEN t.pledged_points + $3 = t.target_points
                             THEN 'funded'::funding_status ELSE t.funding_status END
  FROM wishlist_items s
 WHERE t.id = $2 AND s.id = $1 AND s.wishlist_id = t.wishlist_id AND s.id <> t.id
   AND s.funding_status = 'expired' AND s.expired_at + interval '7 days' > now()
   AND t.funding_mode = 'crowdfund' AND t.funding_status = 'open' AND t.funding_deadline > now()
   AND t.pledged_points + $3 <= t.target_points
RETURNING t.pledged_points, t.funding_status;      -- 0 rows → 409 REALLOCATION_NOT_ALLOWED
UPDATE wishlist_items SET pledged_points = pledged_points - $3 WHERE id = $1;
UPDATE contributions SET status = 'reallocated', reallocated_to_item_id = $2
 WHERE id = $4 AND user_id = $5 AND status = 'pledged' RETURNING id;
INSERT INTO contributions (item_id, wishlist_id, user_id, wallet_id, points, message, is_anonymous)
SELECT $2, wishlist_id, user_id, wallet_id, points, message, is_anonymous FROM contributions WHERE id = $4
RETURNING id;
-- 若目標因此 funded，接續 4.3b ⑤（captured + purchase_orders + outbox）。錢包餘額不變，故不寫 ledger。
COMMIT;

-- 4.3f（含前置鎖定與 4.3R）
-- 前置（獨立語句）：SELECT w.id FROM point_wallets w WHERE w.id IN (SELECT wallet_id FROM contributions WHERE item_id = $1 AND status = 'captured') ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE;
-- 實際花費低於 target：差額依各人認捐比例退回，最大餘數法分配整數點（分配總和恆等於差額），退回「原批次」
--   $1=item_id  $2=actual（實際花費，必須 <= target_points）  $3=merchant_order_id
BEGIN;
WITH o AS (
  UPDATE purchase_orders po SET amount = $2, status = 'placed', merchant_order_id = $3
   WHERE po.item_id = $1 AND po.status = 'pending' AND $2 <= (SELECT target_points FROM wishlist_items WHERE id = $1)
  RETURNING po.id, po.item_id, (SELECT target_points FROM wishlist_items WHERE id = $1) - $2 AS diff
), lk AS (   -- 先依 wallet_id 排序鎖定 contributions，避免與其他交易互鎖
  SELECT c.id FROM contributions c JOIN o ON c.item_id = o.item_id
   WHERE c.status = 'captured' ORDER BY c.wallet_id, c.id FOR UPDATE OF c
), t AS (
  SELECT c.id, c.wallet_id, c.created_at, o.diff, o.id AS order_id,
         (o.diff * c.points) / sum(c.points) OVER () AS base,      -- 無條件捨去的份額
         (o.diff * c.points) % sum(c.points) OVER () AS rem        -- 餘數
    FROM contributions c JOIN lk ON lk.id = c.id, o
), ranked AS (
  SELECT *, row_number() OVER (ORDER BY rem DESC, created_at, id) AS rn,   -- 餘數大者優先，同餘數先認捐者優先
         diff - sum(base) OVER () AS leftover                              -- 尚未分配的點數
    FROM t
), c_upd AS (
  UPDATE contributions c SET refunded_points = c.refunded_points + (r.base + CASE WHEN r.rn <= r.leftover THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
    FROM ranked r WHERE c.id = r.id RETURNING 1
), rel AS (  -- 餘下步驟同 4.3R：逐批次退回、寫 ledger（ref = purchase_order）
  SELECT id AS contribution_id, wallet_id, base + CASE WHEN rn <= leftover THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS back,
         'purchase_order'::text AS ref_type, order_id AS ref_id
    FROM ranked WHERE base + CASE WHEN rn <= leftover THEN 1 ELSE 0 END > 0
)
, lkw AS (                                                       -- 先鎖 wallet（依 id 排序）
  SELECT w.id, w.balance FROM point_wallets w
   WHERE w.id IN (SELECT wallet_id FROM rel) ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE
), alloc AS (
  SELECT r.contribution_id, r.wallet_id, r.back, r.ref_type, r.ref_id, a.lot_id, l.source, l.expires_at,
         a.points - a.refunded_points AS avail,
         sum(a.points - a.refunded_points) OVER o AS cum,
         row_number() OVER o AS ord
    FROM rel r
    JOIN lkw ON lkw.id = r.wallet_id
    JOIN contribution_lot_allocations a ON a.contribution_id = r.contribution_id
    JOIN point_lots l ON l.id = a.lot_id
  WINDOW o AS (PARTITION BY r.contribution_id
               ORDER BY CASE l.source WHEN 'topup' THEN 0 WHEN 'gift_card' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, l.id)
), take AS (
  SELECT *, LEAST(avail, back - (cum - avail)) AS pts,
         (source = 'reward_bonus' AND expires_at <= now()) AS voided
    FROM alloc WHERE avail > 0 AND cum - avail < back
), a_upd AS (
  UPDATE contribution_lot_allocations a SET refunded_points = a.refunded_points + t.pts
    FROM take t WHERE a.contribution_id = t.contribution_id AND a.lot_id = t.lot_id RETURNING 1
), l_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = l.remaining_points + s.pts
    FROM (SELECT lot_id, sum(pts) AS pts FROM take WHERE NOT voided GROUP BY lot_id) s
   WHERE l.id = s.lot_id RETURNING 1
), w_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_wallets pw SET balance = pw.balance + s.pts
    FROM (SELECT wallet_id, sum(pts) AS pts FROM take WHERE NOT voided GROUP BY wallet_id) s
   WHERE pw.id = s.wallet_id RETURNING 1
), ent AS (                                                      -- 每個批次一筆 release；作廢者再補一筆 expire 抵銷
  SELECT wallet_id, lot_id, pts AS delta, 'release'::ledger_entry_type AS entry_type, ref_type, ref_id, contribution_id, ord * 2 AS k FROM take
  UNION ALL
  SELECT wallet_id, lot_id, -pts, 'expire', ref_type, ref_id, contribution_id, ord * 2 + 1 FROM take WHERE voided
), fin AS (                                                      -- 每個 wallet 異動後的餘額
  SELECT lkw.id AS wallet_id,
         lkw.balance + COALESCE((SELECT sum(pts) FROM take t WHERE t.wallet_id = lkw.id AND NOT t.voided), 0) AS balance_after
    FROM lkw
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT e.wallet_id, e.lot_id, e.delta,
       f.balance_after - COALESCE(sum(e.delta) OVER (PARTITION BY e.wallet_id ORDER BY e.contribution_id, e.k
                                                      ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING), 0),
       e.entry_type, e.ref_type, e.ref_id
  FROM ent e JOIN fin f USING (wallet_id)
 ORDER BY e.wallet_id, e.contribution_id, e.k;
COMMIT;
-- 例：target 7600、實際 7500、兩筆 1600 / 6000 → 退 21 / 79，合計恰為差額 100，並退回各自原批次。
-- 實際花費 > target（漲價超過緩衝）不走此路，視為購買失敗，見 4.3g。

-- 4.3g 購買失敗（缺貨、漲價超過緩衝、商家拒單）：所有 captured → released，剩餘未退點數退回「原批次」；已給的星星扣回
BEGIN;
SELECT w.id FROM point_wallets w                                         -- 先鎖 wallet（依 id 排序）
 WHERE w.id IN (SELECT wallet_id FROM contributions WHERE item_id = $1 AND status = 'captured') ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE;
UPDATE purchase_orders SET status = 'failed', failure_reason = $2
 WHERE item_id = $1 AND status IN ('pending', 'placed') RETURNING id;     -- $3 = 此 id
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET pledged_points = 0,                                               -- 全數 released
       funding_status = CASE WHEN i.funding_deadline > now() THEN 'open'::funding_status
                             ELSE 'expired'::funding_status END,          -- 仍在期限內回 open，否則進入 7 天選擇期的 expired
       expired_at     = CASE WHEN i.funding_deadline > now() THEN NULL ELSE now() END
 WHERE i.id = $1;
WITH rel(contribution_id, wallet_id, back, ref_type, ref_id) AS (
  SELECT c.id, c.wallet_id, c.points - c.refunded_points, 'purchase_order', $3::uuid    -- 已因差額退過的部分不再退
    FROM contributions c WHERE c.item_id = $1 AND c.status = 'captured' AND c.points > c.refunded_points
)
-- ↓ 接 4.3R 的語句主體
;
UPDATE contributions SET status = 'released', released_at = now(), refunded_points = points
 WHERE item_id = $1 AND status = 'captured';
INSERT INTO outbox_events (kind, payload) VALUES
  ('rewards.clawback', jsonb_build_object('item_id', $1, 'order_id', $3)),      -- 星星扣回（4.3n），不為負
  ('order.failed.notify', jsonb_build_object('item_id', $1));                     -- 通知建立者與捐贈者
COMMIT;

-- 4.3h 現金退款申請（POST /wallet/cash-refunds）：只能退 cash_refundable 批次（topup）；禮物卡 / 贊助點數不可退現金
--      balance 足夠但可退現金的點數不足 → 409 NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE（整句不扣任何批次）
BEGIN;
-- $1=user_id $2=amount $3=wallet_id $4=wallet 扣點後 balance $5=新 request id
-- ① 先鎖 wallet 並扣點；0 rows → frozen → 403 WALLET_FROZEN，否則 balance 不足 → 409 INSUFFICIENT_POINTS
UPDATE point_wallets SET balance = balance - $2 WHERE user_id = $1 AND balance >= $2 AND status = 'active' RETURNING id, balance;   -- 凍結 → 403 WALLET_FROZEN
-- ② 只從 cash_refundable 批次扣（先進先出）；可退現金的點數不足 → 0 rows → ROLLBACK → 409 NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE
WITH cand AS (
  SELECT l.id, l.remaining_points AS rem,
         sum(l.remaining_points) OVER o AS cum, row_number() OVER o AS ord
    FROM point_lots l
   WHERE l.wallet_id = $3 AND l.cash_refundable AND l.remaining_points > 0
  WINDOW o AS (ORDER BY l.created_at, l.id)
), take AS (
  SELECT id, ord, LEAST(rem, $2 - (cum - rem)) AS pts FROM cand WHERE cum - rem < $2
), ok AS (
  SELECT COALESCE(sum(pts), 0) = $2 AS good FROM take
), upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = l.remaining_points - t.pts
    FROM take t, ok WHERE ok.good AND l.id = t.id RETURNING l.id
), req AS (
  INSERT INTO cash_refund_requests (id, wallet_id, amount, lot_allocations)
  SELECT $5, $3, $2, (SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('lot_id', id, 'points', pts) ORDER BY ord) FROM take)
   WHERE (SELECT good FROM ok) RETURNING id
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $3, t.id, -t.pts,
       $4 + COALESCE(sum(t.pts) OVER (ORDER BY t.ord ROWS BETWEEN 1 FOLLOWING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING), 0),
       'cash_refund', 'cash_refund_request', $5
  FROM take t, ok WHERE ok.good ORDER BY t.ord;
COMMIT;
-- 認捐中（pledged / captured）的點數不在 balance 內，自然不可申請。

-- 4.3i 營運處理現金退款（PATCH /admin/cash-refunds/{id}）：requested → processing → completed；requested / processing → rejected
UPDATE cash_refund_requests SET status = 'processing', processed_by = $2 WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'requested';
UPDATE cash_refund_requests SET status = 'completed', completed_at = now(), processed_by = $2
 WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'processing';
BEGIN;
-- rejected：點數退回「原批次」（依申請時記錄的 lot_allocations），ledger 記 adjustment
WITH r AS (
  UPDATE cash_refund_requests
     SET status = 'rejected', reject_reason = $3, processed_by = $2, completed_at = now()
   WHERE id = $1 AND status IN ('requested', 'processing')
  RETURNING id, wallet_id, amount, lot_allocations
), lkw AS (
  SELECT w.id, w.balance FROM point_wallets w JOIN r ON r.wallet_id = w.id FOR UPDATE OF w
), items AS (
  SELECT r.wallet_id, (e->>'lot_id')::uuid AS lot_id, (e->>'points')::bigint AS pts, x.ord
    FROM r, jsonb_array_elements(r.lot_allocations) WITH ORDINALITY AS x(e, ord)
), l_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = l.remaining_points + i.pts FROM items i WHERE l.id = i.lot_id RETURNING 1
), w_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_wallets pw SET balance = pw.balance + r.amount FROM r WHERE pw.id = r.wallet_id RETURNING 1
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT i.wallet_id, i.lot_id, i.pts,
       lkw.balance + sum(i.pts) OVER (ORDER BY i.ord),
       'adjustment', 'cash_refund_request', (SELECT id FROM r)
  FROM items i JOIN lkw ON lkw.id = i.wallet_id ORDER BY i.ord;
COMMIT;

-- 4.3j 自動下單 job 領單（租約 + 指數退避；catalog）
UPDATE purchase_orders po SET locked_until = now() + interval '2 minutes', attempts = po.attempts + 1
 WHERE po.id IN (
   SELECT id FROM purchase_orders
    WHERE status = 'pending' AND fulfillment_type = 'catalog'
      AND (next_attempt_at IS NULL OR next_attempt_at <= now())
      AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
    ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 10 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED)
RETURNING po.id, po.item_id, po.attempts;
-- 商家 API 暫時失敗：UPDATE purchase_orders SET next_attempt_at = now() + interval '1 minute' * power(2, attempts), locked_until = NULL WHERE id = $1;
-- attempts 達上限（例如 5）或商家明確拒單：視為購買失敗，走 4.3g。

-- 4.3k 公開頁 / SSE 的 display_status（D22-6）：由 funding_status + purchase_orders.status 推導
SELECT i.id,
       CASE i.funding_status
         WHEN 'open'      THEN 'open'
         WHEN 'expired'   THEN 'expired'
         WHEN 'fulfilled' THEN 'delivered'
         WHEN 'funded'    THEN CASE po.status
                                 WHEN 'placed'    THEN 'ordered'
                                 WHEN 'shipped'   THEN 'shipped'
                                 WHEN 'delivered' THEN 'delivered'
                                 ELSE 'funded' END      -- pending（排隊下單中）
       END AS display_status
  FROM wishlist_items i
  LEFT JOIN purchase_orders po ON po.item_id = i.id
 WHERE i.funding_mode = 'crowdfund' AND i.wishlist_id = $1;      -- quantity 品項為 NULL

-- 4.3l 電子禮物卡兌換（POST /gift-cards/claim，D25）：收卡者登入後兌換 → gift_card 批次（不可退現金、不到期）
-- $1=code_hash $2=user_id（Idempotency-Key 與 Turnstile 驗證已在應用層完成）
BEGIN;
UPDATE gift_cards
   SET status = 'claimed', claimed_by_user_id = $2, claimed_at = now(), code_enc = NULL      -- D31-1：兌換後清除暫存密文
 WHERE code_hash = $1 AND status = 'active' AND (claimable_at IS NULL OR claimable_at <= now())   -- D41：高風險訂單的卡延遲兌換
RETURNING id, face_value_points;           -- 0 rows → 查 status：claimed → 409 GIFT_CARD_ALREADY_CLAIMED；不存在 / refunded / void → 404 GIFT_CARD_INVALID
INSERT INTO point_wallets (user_id) VALUES ($2)
ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET updated_at = now() RETURNING id;        -- 取得 / 鎖定 wallet（首次兌換即建立）
UPDATE point_wallets SET balance = balance + $3 WHERE id = $4 RETURNING balance;   -- $3 = face_value_points $4 = wallet_id
WITH lot AS (                              -- gift_card 批次：不可退現金、不到期（只能用於心願品項）
  INSERT INTO point_lots (wallet_id, source, source_ref_id, original_points, remaining_points, cash_refundable)
  VALUES ($4, 'gift_card', $5, $3, $3, false) RETURNING id                         -- $5 = gift_cards.id；point_lots_source_uk 擋重複入帳
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $4, id, $3, $6, 'gift_claim', 'gift_card', $5 FROM lot;
COMMIT;
-- 失敗歸因（rollback 後）；claimable_at 尚未到 → 409 GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE（body 附 claimable_at，D47-1）；錢包 frozen → 403 WALLET_FROZEN
SELECT status, claimable_at FROM gift_cards WHERE code_hash = $1;

-- 4.3m 禮物卡訂單入帳與退款（D25）
-- 禮物卡訂單入帳（webhook / 同步付款成功；provider_txn_id 冪等）：標記 paid 並產生 quantity 張卡
-- 單一語句：標記 paid 與產生卡片同時成功或同時不發生（重送時 UPDATE 0 rows → 不產生任何卡）
--   $1=order_id $2=provider_txn_id $3=total_amount $4=theme $5=message $6=sender_display_name
--   $7=code_hash[]  $8=code_enc[]（應用層產生 quantity 組 16 碼 code：hash = SHA-256(code)，enc = AEAD(code)）
--   D31-1：個人卡的 code_enc 保留到卡片 claimed / refunded / void 才清為 NULL，購買者可隨時用 GET /gift-cards/orders/{id} 重看分享連結（補發）
WITH o AS (
  UPDATE gift_card_orders SET status = 'paid', paid_at = now(), provider_txn_id = $2
   WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'pending' AND total_amount = $3
  RETURNING id, quantity, face_value_points
)
INSERT INTO gift_cards (order_id, code_hash, code_enc, face_value_points, theme, message, sender_display_name)
SELECT o.id, t.h, t.e, o.face_value_points, $4, $5, $6
  FROM o, unnest($7::bytea[], $8::bytea[]) AS t(h, e);

-- 購買者申請退款：僅未兌換（active）可退；已兌換 → 0 rows → 409 GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE
UPDATE gift_cards g SET status = 'refunded', code_enc = NULL        -- D31-1：退款後清除暫存密文
  FROM gift_card_orders o
 WHERE g.id = $1 AND g.order_id = o.id AND o.purchaser_user_id = $2 AND g.status = 'active'
RETURNING g.id, g.face_value_points;
-- 成功後寫 outbox（kind = giftcard.refund）→ job 原路退款；整張訂單的卡都 refunded 時訂單轉 refunded

-- 4.3n 回饋星星（D26）：賺取 / 扣回由 outbox job 執行（冪等），兌換在交易內
-- 星星賺取（outbox 任務 rewards.earn，認捐 captured 後非同步；D31-5）
--   給星點數 = 該筆 captured 認捐中「來自 gift_card + topup 批次」的 allocations 加總；以 reward_bonus 批次支付的點數不給星；每 50 點 1 星（無條件捨去）
--   儲值與購買禮物卡本身不給星
INSERT INTO reward_accounts (user_id)
SELECT DISTINCT user_id FROM contributions WHERE id = ANY($1) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
WITH c AS (
  SELECT c.id, c.user_id,
         COALESCE((SELECT sum(a.points) FROM contribution_lot_allocations a JOIN point_lots l ON l.id = a.lot_id
                    WHERE a.contribution_id = c.id AND l.source IN ('gift_card', 'topup')), 0) / 50 AS stars
    FROM contributions c WHERE c.id = ANY($1) AND c.status = 'captured'
), e AS (
  INSERT INTO star_ledger (user_id, delta, reason, ref_type, ref_id, expires_at)
  SELECT user_id, stars, 'earn', 'contribution', id,
         (date_trunc('month', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei') + interval '13 months') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei'   -- 賺取月份起算 12 個月（月底到期）
    FROM c WHERE stars > 0
  ON CONFLICT (reason, ref_type, ref_id) WHERE reason IN ('earn', 'clawback') DO NOTHING      -- job 重跑冪等
  RETURNING user_id, delta
)
UPDATE reward_accounts ra SET stars_balance = ra.stars_balance + s.stars
  FROM (SELECT user_id, sum(delta) AS stars FROM e GROUP BY user_id) s WHERE ra.user_id = s.user_id;

-- 購買失敗：已給的星星扣回；餘額不足則扣到 0 為止（不得為負），實扣數記入 star_ledger，短少部分由應用層寫 audit_logs
WITH e AS (
  SELECT user_id, sum(delta) AS owed FROM star_ledger
   WHERE reason = 'earn' AND ref_type = 'contribution' AND ref_id = ANY($1) GROUP BY user_id
), cur AS (
  SELECT ra.user_id, e.owed, LEAST(ra.stars_balance, e.owed) AS actual
    FROM reward_accounts ra JOIN e USING (user_id) ORDER BY ra.user_id FOR UPDATE OF ra
), u AS (
  UPDATE reward_accounts ra SET stars_balance = ra.stars_balance - c.actual FROM cur c WHERE ra.user_id = c.user_id RETURNING ra.user_id
)
INSERT INTO star_ledger (user_id, delta, reason, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT c.user_id, -c.actual, 'clawback', 'purchase_order', $2 FROM cur c WHERE c.actual > 0;      -- $2 = purchase_orders.id

-- POST /rewards/redemptions {offer_id}；鎖順序：wallet → reward_accounts（與認捐路徑一致）
BEGIN;
INSERT INTO point_wallets (user_id) VALUES ($1) ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET updated_at = now() RETURNING id;   -- wallet（鎖）
UPDATE reward_accounts ra SET stars_balance = ra.stars_balance - o.stars_cost
  FROM reward_offers o
 WHERE ra.user_id = $1 AND o.id = $2 AND o.active AND o.tier_required = ra.tier AND ra.stars_balance >= o.stars_cost
RETURNING o.stars_cost, o.bonus_points, ra.stars_balance;      -- 0 rows → 409 INSUFFICIENT_STARS（或 offer 不存在 / 不適用 → 404 / 422）
UPDATE point_wallets SET balance = balance + $3 WHERE id = $4 RETURNING balance;    -- $3 = bonus_points $4 = wallet_id
WITH lot AS (                                                   -- reward_bonus 批次：不可退現金、90 天到期、非預收款
  INSERT INTO point_lots (wallet_id, source, source_ref_id, original_points, remaining_points, cash_refundable, expires_at)
  VALUES ($4, 'reward_bonus', $2, $3, $3, false, now() + interval '90 days') RETURNING id
), sl AS (
  INSERT INTO star_ledger (user_id, delta, reason, ref_type, ref_id) SELECT $1, -$5, 'redeem', 'reward_offer', $2 FROM lot RETURNING 1   -- $5 = stars_cost
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $4, id, $3, $6, 'reward_bonus', 'reward_offer', $2 FROM lot;
COMMIT;

-- 星星到期（每日）：到期的 earn 批次，扣除已被兌換 / 扣回 / 到期消耗的部分（先進先出）後，剩餘者失效
WITH x AS (
  SELECT user_id,
         COALESCE(sum(delta) FILTER (WHERE reason = 'earn' AND expires_at <= now()), 0)                  AS earned_expired,
         COALESCE(-sum(delta) FILTER (WHERE reason IN ('redeem', 'clawback', 'expire')), 0)               AS consumed
    FROM star_ledger GROUP BY user_id
), cur AS (
  SELECT ra.user_id, LEAST(ra.stars_balance, GREATEST(x.earned_expired - x.consumed, 0)) AS n
    FROM reward_accounts ra JOIN x USING (user_id) ORDER BY ra.user_id FOR UPDATE OF ra
), u AS (
  UPDATE reward_accounts ra SET stars_balance = ra.stars_balance - c.n FROM cur c WHERE ra.user_id = c.user_id AND c.n > 0 RETURNING ra.user_id
)
INSERT INTO star_ledger (user_id, delta, reason, ref_type) SELECT user_id, -n, 'expire', 'manual' FROM cur WHERE n > 0;

-- 等級評估（每日）：12 個月內賺取 ≥ 300 星為 gold
UPDATE reward_accounts ra
   SET tier = CASE WHEN s.earned >= 300 THEN 'gold'::reward_tier ELSE 'green'::reward_tier END, tier_evaluated_at = now()
  FROM (SELECT ra2.user_id,
               COALESCE((SELECT sum(delta) FROM star_ledger sl
                          WHERE sl.user_id = ra2.user_id AND sl.reason = 'earn' AND sl.created_at > now() - interval '12 months'), 0) AS earned
          FROM reward_accounts ra2) s
 WHERE ra.user_id = s.user_id;

-- 4.3o 點數批次到期 job（D23，每日）
-- 每日：到期的 reward_bonus 批次 → expire（餘額歸零、扣 wallet.balance、ledger expire）；先鎖 wallet 再動批次
WITH lkw AS (
  SELECT w.id, w.balance FROM point_wallets w
   WHERE w.id IN (SELECT wallet_id FROM point_lots WHERE source = 'reward_bonus' AND expires_at <= now() AND remaining_points > 0)
   ORDER BY w.id FOR UPDATE
), due AS (
  SELECT l.id AS lot_id, l.wallet_id, l.remaining_points AS pts,
         row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY l.wallet_id ORDER BY l.expires_at, l.id) AS ord
    FROM point_lots l JOIN lkw ON lkw.id = l.wallet_id
   WHERE l.source = 'reward_bonus' AND l.expires_at <= now() AND l.remaining_points > 0
), l_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = 0 FROM due d WHERE l.id = d.lot_id RETURNING 1
), w_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_wallets pw SET balance = pw.balance - s.pts
    FROM (SELECT wallet_id, sum(pts) AS pts FROM due GROUP BY wallet_id) s WHERE pw.id = s.wallet_id RETURNING 1
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT d.wallet_id, d.lot_id, -d.pts,
       lkw.balance - sum(d.pts) OVER (PARTITION BY d.wallet_id ORDER BY d.ord),
       'expire', 'point_lot', d.lot_id
  FROM due d JOIN lkw ON lkw.id = d.wallet_id ORDER BY d.wallet_id, d.ord;

-- 4.3p 自動儲值（D24）
-- 認捐成功後（同交易內）：餘額低於門檻 → 寫 outbox，由 job 非同步扣卡；認捐本身不等待扣款
INSERT INTO outbox_events (kind, payload)
SELECT 'wallet.auto_topup', jsonb_build_object('user_id', a.user_id, 'amount', a.amount)
  FROM wallet_auto_topups a
 WHERE a.user_id = $1 AND a.enabled AND $2 < a.threshold_points;      -- $2 = 認捐後 wallet.balance

-- job：當月（Asia/Taipei）自動儲值累計 + 本次 <= monthly_cap_amount 才扣款；超過 → 不扣、發通知（payload.code = AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED），不阻擋認捐
SELECT a.amount, a.monthly_cap_amount,
       COALESCE((SELECT sum(t.amount) FROM topups t
                  WHERE t.wallet_id = $2 AND t.is_auto AND t.status IN ('pending', 'paid')
                    AND t.created_at >= date_trunc('month', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei'), 0) AS used_this_month
  FROM wallet_auto_topups a WHERE a.user_id = $1 AND a.enabled;

-- 4.3q 錢包分批餘額
-- GET /wallet 分批餘額
SELECT w.balance,
       COALESCE(sum(l.remaining_points) FILTER (WHERE l.cash_refundable), 0)                      AS refundable_points,
       COALESCE(sum(l.remaining_points) FILTER (WHERE l.source = 'gift_card'), 0)                  AS gift_points,
       COALESCE(sum(l.remaining_points) FILTER (WHERE l.source = 'reward_bonus'
                                                  AND (l.expires_at IS NULL OR l.expires_at > now())), 0) AS bonus_points,
       COALESCE(sum(l.remaining_points) FILTER (WHERE l.source = 'reward_bonus'
                                                  AND l.expires_at > now() AND l.expires_at <= now() + interval '14 days'), 0) AS bonus_expiring_soon,
       min(l.expires_at) FILTER (WHERE l.source = 'reward_bonus' AND l.remaining_points > 0)        AS bonus_next_expiry
  FROM point_wallets w
  LEFT JOIN point_lots l ON l.wallet_id = w.id AND l.remaining_points > 0
 WHERE w.user_id = $1
 GROUP BY w.id;

-- 4.3r 企業禮券批次：CSV 匯出（只能一次）與補發（D27 / D31-1 / D31-8）
-- 企業禮券批次：匯出兌換連結 CSV（GET /admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/codes）——只能一次
BEGIN;
UPDATE gift_card_batches SET codes_exported_at = now()
 WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'issued' AND codes_exported_at IS NULL
RETURNING id;                                  -- 0 rows → 已匯出過 → 409 CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED（批次非 issued → 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION）
UPDATE gift_cards SET code_enc = NULL
 WHERE batch_id = $1 AND code_enc IS NOT NULL
RETURNING id, code_enc;                        -- 應用層解密這些密文組成 CSV；D31-1：匯出後即清為 NULL
COMMIT;

-- 補發（POST /admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/reissue，D31-8）：CSV 遺失時
--   該批次所有 active 未兌換卡 void，產生同數量新卡，codes_exported_at 重置為 NULL，可再匯出一次；已兌換的卡不受影響
--   $1=batch_id  $2=新卡 code_hash[]  $3=新卡 code_enc[]（數量須等於被 void 的張數，由應用層先 SELECT count 後產生）
BEGIN;
SELECT id FROM gift_card_batches WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'issued' FOR UPDATE;
WITH v AS (
  UPDATE gift_cards SET status = 'void', code_enc = NULL
   WHERE batch_id = $1 AND status = 'active' RETURNING face_value_points
), ins AS (
  INSERT INTO gift_cards (batch_id, code_hash, code_enc, face_value_points, theme)
  SELECT $1, t.h, t.e, (SELECT face_value_points FROM gift_card_batches WHERE id = $1), 'corporate'
    FROM unnest($2::bytea[], $3::bytea[]) AS t(h, e)
   WHERE array_length($2::bytea[], 1) = (SELECT count(*) FROM v)          -- 張數不符就不產生（整筆回滾由應用層處理）
  RETURNING 1
)
UPDATE gift_card_batches SET codes_exported_at = NULL WHERE id = $1 RETURNING id;
COMMIT;

-- 4.3s 信用卡拒付 / 盜刷（D41）：金流 webhook 回報爭議
-- (1) 爭議開始：topups.status = disputed，凍結錢包（認捐 / 兌換 / 退款 → 403 WALLET_FROZEN）
UPDATE topups SET status = 'disputed' WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'paid' RETURNING wallet_id, amount;      -- $2 = wallet_id
UPDATE point_wallets SET status = 'frozen' WHERE id = $2;
-- (2) 爭議敗訴（charged_back）：先扣回該 topup 批次「剩餘」點數；已花掉的部分記入 wallet_debts（balance 仍 >= 0）
--     $1=topup_id  $2=wallet_id  $3=topup.amount
BEGIN;
SELECT id FROM point_wallets WHERE id = $2 FOR UPDATE;                                                     -- wallet 先鎖
WITH t AS (                                                                                                -- 0 rows → 已處理，下列全部不動作（冪等）
  UPDATE topups SET status = 'charged_back' WHERE id = $1 AND status IN ('paid', 'disputed') RETURNING id
), lot AS (
  SELECT l.id, l.remaining_points AS pts FROM point_lots l JOIN t ON l.source_ref_id = t.id WHERE l.source = 'topup' FOR UPDATE OF l
), l_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_lots l SET remaining_points = 0 FROM lot WHERE l.id = lot.id RETURNING lot.pts
), w_upd AS (
  UPDATE point_wallets pw SET balance = pw.balance - (SELECT COALESCE(sum(pts), 0) FROM l_upd), status = 'frozen'
   WHERE pw.id = $2 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t) RETURNING pw.balance
), debt AS (                                  -- 不足部分（已被認捐 / 用掉的點數）記欠款，平台吸收損失
  INSERT INTO wallet_debts (wallet_id, topup_id, amount)
  SELECT $2, $1, $3 - COALESCE((SELECT sum(pts) FROM l_upd), 0)
   WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t) AND $3 - COALESCE((SELECT sum(pts) FROM l_upd), 0) > 0
  RETURNING amount
)
INSERT INTO point_ledger (wallet_id, lot_id, delta, balance_after, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)
SELECT $2, lot.id, -lot.pts, (SELECT balance FROM w_upd), 'adjustment', 'topup', $1
  FROM lot WHERE lot.pts > 0;
-- 禮物卡訂單拒付：未兌換卡 void（連同 code_enc 清除）；已兌換不追回收卡者，平台吸收並封鎖購買者
UPDATE gift_cards SET status = 'void', code_enc = NULL WHERE order_id = $4 AND status = 'active';
UPDATE users SET blocked_at = now() WHERE id = $5 AND blocked_at IS NULL;                                   -- $5 = 購買者
INSERT INTO audit_logs (actor_type, action, entity, entity_id, diff) VALUES ('system', 'topup.charged_back', 'topups', $1, jsonb_build_object('amount', $3));
COMMIT;
-- (3) 爭議勝訴：UPDATE topups SET status = 'paid' WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'disputed'; UPDATE point_wallets SET status = 'active' WHERE id = $2;
-- 已用於達標下單的點數不追回受捐者的禮物。

-- 4.9 帳號刪除與去識別化（DELETE /me，D40；二次確認在應用層）
--     $1 = user_id
BEGIN;
-- P2 前置檢查（任一成立 → ROLLBACK → 409 ACCOUNT_HAS_BALANCE；點數須先申請現金退款或明確放棄）：
SELECT (SELECT COALESCE(sum(balance), 0) FROM point_wallets WHERE user_id = $1) AS balance,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM contributions WHERE user_id = $1 AND status = 'pledged') AS pledged,
       (SELECT count(*) FROM purchase_orders po JOIN contributions c ON c.item_id = po.item_id
         WHERE c.user_id = $1 AND po.status IN ('pending', 'placed', 'shipped')) AS open_orders;
UPDATE sessions SET revoked_at = now() WHERE user_id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL;
DELETE FROM otp_challenges WHERE email = (SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = $1);
DELETE FROM auth_identities WHERE user_id = $1;
UPDATE wishlists SET status = 'archived', visibility = 'private', closed_at = COALESCE(closed_at, now())
 WHERE owner_id = $1 AND status <> 'archived';                                  -- 清單封存並從公開移除
UPDATE claims SET claimer_name = '已刪除的使用者' WHERE user_id = $1;             -- 在他人清單上的認領保留，去識別
UPDATE notifications SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE user_id = $1 AND status = 'pending';
UPDATE users
   SET display_name = '已刪除的使用者', email = NULL, avatar_key = NULL,
       notification_prefs = '{}'::jsonb, deleted_at = now(), anonymized_at = now()
 WHERE id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL;
INSERT INTO audit_logs (actor_type, actor_id, action, entity, entity_id) VALUES ('user', $1, 'account.delete', 'users', $1);
COMMIT;
-- 金融紀錄（point_ledger、topups、invoices、purchase_orders）依商業會計法保存並以 user_id 假名化，不刪除（年限待會計師確認）。

-- 訪客刪除（DELETE /guest/me）：清除暱稱 / 聯絡方式 / email，claims 保留為「已刪除的訪客」
--     $1 = guest_id
UPDATE guests SET display_name = '已刪除的訪客', contact = NULL, email = NULL, deleted_at = now()
 WHERE id = $1 AND deleted_at IS NULL;
UPDATE claims SET claimer_name = '已刪除的訪客' WHERE guest_id = $1;
UPDATE notifications SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE guest_id = $1 AND status = 'pending';
-- 之後帶該 token 的請求 → 401（應用層檢查 guests.deleted_at IS NOT NULL）

-- 4.10 MVP Email 通知（D39）：認領成功交易內寫入 notifications（outbox）
--   第一筆即時（claim.created）；之後每日彙整一封（claim.digest，累加 count）；驚喜模式內容只寫「有 N 件新認領」
--   $1 = wishlist_id  $2 = 下一個台北 09:00 的 timestamptz
INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, channel, kind, payload, scheduled_at)
SELECT u.id, 'email',
       CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM notifications n
                              WHERE n.user_id = u.id AND n.kind = 'claim.created' AND n.payload->>'wishlist_id' = w.id::text)
            THEN 'claim.created' ELSE 'claim.digest' END,
       jsonb_build_object('wishlist_id', w.id, 'count', 1),
       CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM notifications n
                              WHERE n.user_id = u.id AND n.kind = 'claim.created' AND n.payload->>'wishlist_id' = w.id::text)
            THEN now() ELSE $2 END
  FROM wishlists w JOIN users u ON u.id = w.owner_id
 WHERE w.id = $1 AND u.deleted_at IS NULL AND COALESCE((u.notification_prefs->>'email_claims')::boolean, true)
ON CONFLICT (user_id, kind, (payload->>'wishlist_id')) WHERE kind = 'claim.digest' AND status = 'pending'
DO UPDATE SET payload = jsonb_set(notifications.payload, '{count}', to_jsonb((notifications.payload->>'count')::int + 1));
-- payload 不含品項與認領者；寄信時若 surprise_locked 只渲染「有 N 件新認領」。

-- 4.11 檢舉與下架（D35 / D36）
-- 公開頁歸因：hidden → 410 WISHLIST_REMOVED；draft / archived / private / 不存在 → 404
SELECT w.id, w.moderation_status FROM wishlists w
 WHERE w.slug = $1 AND w.deleted_at IS NULL AND w.visibility <> 'private' AND w.status IN ('active', 'closed');
-- staff 下架 / 恢復（同交易寫 audit_logs，actor_type = staff）
BEGIN;
UPDATE wishlists
   SET moderation_status = $2, moderation_reason = CASE WHEN $2 = 'hidden' THEN $3 ELSE NULL END,
       moderated_at = now(), moderated_by = $4
 WHERE id = $1 RETURNING id, moderation_status;
INSERT INTO audit_logs (actor_type, actor_id, action, entity, entity_id, diff)
VALUES ('staff', $4, 'wishlist.moderate', 'wishlists', $1, jsonb_build_object('status', $2, 'reason', $3));
-- 同一清單所有 open 檢舉一併處理
UPDATE content_reports SET status = 'actioned', handled_by = $4, handled_at = now()
 WHERE wishlist_id = $1 AND status = 'open' AND $2 = 'hidden';
COMMIT;

-- 4.12 訪客恢復權杖（D47-3）：寄信時產生新權杖（每次寄信一個新的），信件連結 /me/claims#r={token}
--   寄信：INSERT INTO guest_recovery_tokens (guest_id, token_hash, expires_at) VALUES ($1, $2, now() + interval '30 days');
-- POST /guest/recover {token}：單一語句驗證 + 標記已用 + 換發新的 guest token（舊 token 同時失效）
--   $1 = SHA-256(token)  $2 = SHA-256(新 guest token)
WITH t AS (
  UPDATE guest_recovery_tokens SET used_at = now()
   WHERE token_hash = $1 AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now()
  RETURNING guest_id
)
UPDATE guests g SET guest_token_hash = $2, last_seen_at = now()
  FROM t WHERE g.id = t.guest_id AND g.deleted_at IS NULL
RETURNING g.id, g.display_name;
-- 0 rows（無效 / 已用 / 過期 / 訪客已刪除）→ 一律 404 NOT_FOUND；成功回新的 guest token 並 Set-Cookie ws_guest

-- 4.13 一鍵退訂（GET /unsubscribe?token=，D47-4）：token = HMAC-SHA256 簽章，含 user_id | guest_id 與通知類型
--   驗簽通過後：
-- 建立者：關閉該類通知（鍵名 email_claims）
UPDATE users SET notification_prefs = jsonb_set(notification_prefs, '{email_claims}', 'false'::jsonb) WHERE id = $1;
UPDATE notifications SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE user_id = $1 AND status = 'pending' AND kind IN ('claim.created', 'claim.digest');
-- 訪客：不再寄信（清除 email；管理連結改由訪客日後再次留 Email 取得）
UPDATE guests SET email = NULL WHERE id = $2;
UPDATE notifications SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE guest_id = $2 AND status = 'pending';

-- 4.14 新帳號每日上限（D41 / D47-2）：註冊 30 天內，手動儲值 + 自動儲值 + 禮物卡購買 合計每日（Asia/Taipei）上限 NT$3,000；不含營運建立的企業禮券
--   $1 = user_id  $2 = 本次要建立的金額
SELECT 3000 AS limit_amount,
       3000 - (COALESCE((SELECT sum(t.amount) FROM topups t JOIN point_wallets w ON w.id = t.wallet_id
                          WHERE w.user_id = $1 AND t.status IN ('pending', 'paid')
                            AND t.created_at >= date_trunc('day', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei'), 0)
             + COALESCE((SELECT sum(o.total_amount) FROM gift_card_orders o
                          WHERE o.purchaser_user_id = $1 AND o.status IN ('pending', 'paid')
                            AND o.created_at >= date_trunc('day', now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei') AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei'), 0)) AS remaining_amount
  FROM users u WHERE u.id = $1 AND u.created_at > now() - interval '30 days';
-- 回傳 0 rows = 不是新帳號，不受限；remaining_amount < $2 → 409 TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED（body 附 limit_amount、remaining_amount），直接拒絕。
-- 異常行為（短時間多卡、多帳號同裝置）另由風控標記進人工審核，不影響此錯誤碼。自動儲值 job 超限時不扣款並通知（payload.code = TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED）。

-- 4.15 營運：錢包解凍 / 凍結與欠款結案（P2-A，STAFF_ONLY；皆寫 audit_logs，actor_type = staff）
--   PATCH /admin/wallets/{id} {status, reason}   $1 = wallet_id $2 = 'active' | 'frozen' $3 = staff user_id $4 = reason
BEGIN;
UPDATE point_wallets SET status = $2 WHERE id = $1 RETURNING id, status;
INSERT INTO audit_logs (actor_type, actor_id, action, entity, entity_id, diff)
VALUES ('staff', $3, 'wallet.set_status', 'point_wallets', $1, jsonb_build_object('status', $2, 'reason', $4));
COMMIT;
--   GET /admin/wallet-debts?status=
SELECT d.id, d.amount, d.status, d.created_at, w.user_id, u.display_name, u.email, d.topup_id
  FROM wallet_debts d JOIN point_wallets w ON w.id = d.wallet_id JOIN users u ON u.id = w.user_id
 WHERE d.status = $1 ORDER BY d.created_at LIMIT $2;
--   PATCH /admin/wallet-debts/{id} {status: settled | written_off, note}   $1 = id $2 = status $3 = note $4 = staff
BEGIN;
UPDATE wallet_debts SET status = $2, note = $3, resolved_by = $4, resolved_at = now()
 WHERE id = $1 AND status = 'open' RETURNING id, status;          -- 0 rows → 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION（已結案）
INSERT INTO audit_logs (actor_type, actor_id, action, entity, entity_id, diff)
VALUES ('staff', $4, 'wallet_debt.resolve', 'wallet_debts', $1, jsonb_build_object('status', $2, 'note', $3));
COMMIT;

-- ================================================================
-- 4.4 PostGIS 據點查詢（僅公開、進行中的據點）
-- ================================================================
-- bbox：&& 走 GiST 索引（geography 的 && 比較 2D 邊界框）；ST_MakeEnvelope 參數順序 (minLng, minLat, maxLng, maxLat)
SELECT w.id, w.slug, w.title, w.address, w.site_status,
       ST_Y(w.location::geometry) AS lat, ST_X(w.location::geometry) AS lng
  FROM wishlists w
 WHERE w.type = 'relief' AND w.visibility = 'public' AND w.status = 'active' AND w.deleted_at IS NULL
   AND w.location && ST_MakeEnvelope($1, $2, $3, $4, 4326)::geography
 ORDER BY w.updated_at DESC
 LIMIT $5;

-- near：API 的 near=lat,lng → ST_MakePoint(lng, lat)；半徑單位為公尺
SELECT w.id, w.slug, w.title,
       ST_Distance(w.location, p.pt)::int AS distance_m
  FROM wishlists w,
       LATERAL (SELECT ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint($2, $1), 4326)::geography AS pt) p   -- $1=lat, $2=lng
 WHERE w.type = 'relief' AND w.visibility = 'public' AND w.status = 'active' AND w.deleted_at IS NULL
   AND ST_DWithin(w.location, p.pt, $3 * 1000)                                       -- $3=radius_km
 ORDER BY distance_m
 LIMIT $4;

-- ================================================================
-- 4.5 需求彙總（GET /public/relief/needs/summary?bbox=...）
-- ================================================================
SELECT i.category, i.urgency,
       count(DISTINCT w.id)                              AS site_count,
       sum(i.qty_needed)                                 AS qty_needed,
       sum(i.qty_claimed)                                AS qty_claimed,
       sum(i.qty_received)                               AS qty_received,
       sum(GREATEST(i.qty_needed - i.qty_received, 0))   AS qty_remaining
  FROM wishlist_items i
  JOIN wishlists w ON w.id = i.wishlist_id
 WHERE w.type = 'relief' AND w.visibility = 'public' AND w.status = 'active'
   AND w.site_status = 'active' AND w.deleted_at IS NULL AND i.deleted_at IS NULL
   AND w.location && ST_MakeEnvelope($1, $2, $3, $4, 4326)::geography
 GROUP BY i.category, i.urgency
 ORDER BY i.urgency, i.category;          -- enum 宣告順序：critical 最前

-- ================================================================
-- 4.6 驚喜鎖定判斷（unlock_at = event_date 當日 00:00 Asia/Taipei）
-- ================================================================
SELECT w.id,
       (w.surprise_mode AND (now() AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Taipei')::date < w.event_date) AS surprise_locked
  FROM wishlists w WHERE w.id = $1;
-- 擁有者端 API 在 surprise_locked=true 時：不查 claims / contributions 明細，item 的快取欄位回 null

-- ================================================================
-- 4.7 qty_claimed / pledged_points / 錢包 對帳（每日排程；有差異即告警）
-- ================================================================
SELECT i.id, i.qty_claimed AS cached, COALESCE(s.total, 0) AS actual
  FROM wishlist_items i
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT item_id, sum(qty) AS total
               FROM claims WHERE status IN ('reserved','purchased','delivered')
              GROUP BY item_id) s ON s.item_id = i.id
 WHERE i.funding_mode = 'quantity'
   AND i.qty_claimed IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(s.total, 0);

-- 品項 pledged_points 快取
SELECT i.id, i.pledged_points AS cached,
       COALESCE(sum(c.points) FILTER (WHERE c.status IN ('pledged', 'captured')), 0) AS actual
  FROM wishlist_items i
  LEFT JOIN contributions c ON c.item_id = i.id
 WHERE i.funding_mode = 'crowdfund'
 GROUP BY i.id
HAVING i.pledged_points <> COALESCE(sum(c.points) FILTER (WHERE c.status IN ('pledged', 'captured')), 0);

-- 4.7a 點數批次對帳（D23）
-- 點數批次對帳（每日）：wallet.balance 必須等於 Σ lots.remaining_points，且等於帳本加總與最後一筆 balance_after
SELECT w.id, w.balance, COALESCE(s.lots, 0) AS lots_sum, COALESCE(g.total, 0) AS ledger_sum, l.balance_after AS last_balance_after
  FROM point_wallets w
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT wallet_id, sum(remaining_points) AS lots FROM point_lots GROUP BY wallet_id) s ON s.wallet_id = w.id
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT wallet_id, sum(delta) AS total FROM point_ledger GROUP BY wallet_id) g ON g.wallet_id = w.id
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT balance_after FROM point_ledger WHERE wallet_id = w.id ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1) l ON true
 WHERE w.balance <> COALESCE(s.lots, 0) OR w.balance <> COALESCE(g.total, 0) OR w.balance <> COALESCE(l.balance_after, 0);

-- 批次層：每個批次的 remaining_points 必須等於該批次所有帳本異動加總（每個批次異動都有 lot_id；作廢的 release 與 expire 互相抵銷）
SELECT l.id, l.remaining_points, COALESCE(sum(p.delta), 0) AS ledger_sum
  FROM point_lots l LEFT JOIN point_ledger p ON p.lot_id = l.id
 GROUP BY l.id, l.remaining_points
HAVING l.remaining_points <> COALESCE(sum(p.delta), 0);

-- 認捐佔用：每筆 pledged / captured 的 points - refunded_points 必須等於其 allocations 未退回部分
SELECT c.id FROM contributions c
  LEFT JOIN contribution_lot_allocations a ON a.contribution_id = c.id
 WHERE c.status IN ('pledged', 'captured')
 GROUP BY c.id, c.points, c.refunded_points
HAVING c.points - c.refunded_points <> COALESCE(sum(a.points - a.refunded_points), 0);

-- 儲值批次對帳：Σ topup 批次 original_points = 已入帳儲值總額
SELECT (SELECT COALESCE(sum(original_points), 0) FROM point_lots WHERE source = 'topup') AS topup_lots,
       (SELECT COALESCE(sum(amount), 0) FROM topups WHERE status = 'paid') AS topups_paid;

-- 修復（確認為快取漂移、非資料遺失後才執行）
UPDATE wishlist_items i
   SET qty_claimed = COALESCE((SELECT sum(qty) FROM claims
                                WHERE item_id = i.id AND status IN ('reserved','purchased','delivered')), 0)
 WHERE i.id = $1;

-- ================================================================
-- 4.7b 志工實收（D10：client_request_id 永久去重）
-- ================================================================
BEGIN;
INSERT INTO receipts (item_id, wishlist_id, claim_id, client_request_id, recorded_by, qty, note, received_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)
ON CONFLICT (client_request_id) DO NOTHING
RETURNING id;                       -- 0 rows → 已處理過：SELECT 既有 receipt 原樣回傳，不再累加
UPDATE wishlist_items SET qty_received = qty_received + $6 WHERE id = $1;   -- 僅在上一句有 row 時執行
COMMIT;

-- ================================================================
-- 4.7c 唯讀模式（D9）
-- ================================================================
SELECT (value)::boolean AS read_only FROM system_flags WHERE key = 'read_only';   -- middleware 每請求讀（記憶體快取 ≤ 5 秒）
UPDATE system_flags SET value = 'true'::jsonb WHERE key = 'read_only';           -- 進入唯讀；維護後改回 false

-- ================================================================
-- 4.8 排程清理
-- ================================================================
DELETE FROM idempotency_keys WHERE created_at < now() - interval '24 hours';
DELETE FROM otp_challenges   WHERE expires_at < now() - interval '1 day';
DELETE FROM sessions         WHERE expires_at < now() OR revoked_at < now() - interval '7 days';

5. API Contract

5.1 通用規範

項目規範
Base URLhttps://api.wishsync.tw/api/v1（下文路徑皆省略前綴；每個端點標題完整列出）
Content-Typerequest / response 皆為 application/json; charset=utf-8；錯誤為 application/problem+json；SSE 為 text/event-stream
端點總數88 個（87 個公開 + 1 個內部）：MVP：Auth 6、Wishlist 6、Items 4、Uploads/Link preview 2、Public 2、Claims 3、Guest 2、Account 6（含 POST /guest/recover、GET /unsubscribe）、Moderation 7；P2-D：Relief 11（含組織驗證 2）；P2-A：Wallet 4、Auto top-up 3、Webhooks 1、Contributions 3、Catalog 2、Fulfillment 6、Config 1、Admin wallets 3；P2-B：Gift cards 6、Rewards 3；P2-C：Corporate 6；內部 1

認證

機制載體適用說明
建立者 sessionCookie ws_sessionAuth 之外的建立者 / 組織端點opaque token；HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax；Path=/；30 天滑動；DB 存 SHA-256。非 GET 請求另驗證 Origin 為 https://app.wishsync.tw（CSRF 縱深防禦）
訪客 tokenCookie ws_guest 或 header X-Guest-Tokenclaims、guest 端點（點數認捐、錢包需登入，不適用）32 bytes 隨機（base64url）；首次認領時由伺服器發放，回應 body 的 guest_token 供前端存 localStorage；LINE in-app browser cookie 不穩，因此 header 與 cookie 同時支援，header 優先；DB 僅存 SHA-256（guests.guest_token_hash）；cookie 為 HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Max-Age=1 年
公開無/public/*不讀取任何 cookie，回應不得 Set-Cookie
營運人員Cookie ws_session 且 users.is_staff = true/admin/*（P2）非 staff 回 403 STAFF_ONLY；staff 讀取收件明文會寫 audit_logs
金流簽章供應商簽章 / 反查/webhooks/payments/{provider}（P2）不使用 session，不套用 CORS；驗簽方式見該端點
排程Cloud Scheduler OIDC/internal/jobs/tick不經 Cloudflare、不在公開 API

Idempotency-Key

  • 所有建立型 POST（/items/{item_id}/claims、/items/{item_id}/contributions、/items/{item_id}/receipts、/wallet/topups、/wallet/cash-refunds、/contributions/{id}/reallocate）必須帶 Idempotency-Key: <UUID>，缺少回 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED。
  • 伺服器以 (scope, key) 保存 24 小時；scope = 主體（user / guest / IP）+ method + 路由。同 key 同 body → 重放原回應並加 Idempotency-Replayed: true；同 key 不同 body → 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT；仍在處理中 → 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT + Retry-After: 1。
  • key 與業務寫入在同一交易內佔用，因此失敗（含 4xx）會一併 rollback、key 釋放，可用同一把 key 重試；僅 2xx 回應會被保存重放。
  • 前端需在「同一次使用者操作」的重試之間沿用同一把 key（離線佇列補送亦然）。
  • Webhook 不使用 Idempotency-Key，冪等由 topups.provider_txn_id UNIQUE 保證。

限流標頭

採 IETF RateLimit header fields：RateLimit-Policy、RateLimit-Limit、RateLimit-Remaining、RateLimit-Reset（秒）。超限回 429 與 Retry-After。邊緣由 Cloudflare 速率限制，應用層另以 IP / guest / user 為 key 限流。

範圍建議上限（可調）
POST /auth/otp/request每 email 5 / 小時；每 IP 20 / 小時
POST /auth/otp/verify每 email 10 / 小時（且單一挑戰最多 5 次錯誤）
POST claims每 IP 30 / 分鐘；每 guest 60 / 小時
POST contributions / reallocate每 user 30 / 分鐘
POST /wallet/topups、/gift-cards/orders每 user 10 / 小時（防盜刷測卡）
POST /public/wishlists/{slug}/reports每 IP 5 / 小時；必帶 Turnstile
GET /me/export每帳號 3 / 日
POST /guest/recover每 IP 10 / 10 分鐘
GET /unsubscribe每 IP 60 / 分鐘（簽章無效一律 302 ?ok=0）
POST /gift-cards/claim每 IP 10 / 10 分鐘；每帳號 5 / 小時；必帶 Turnstile（防暴力猜碼）
GET /public/gift-cards/{code}每 IP 30 / 分鐘；失敗過多封鎖；不快取
GET /public/*、/config/public每 IP 120 / 分鐘（多數由 CDN 吸收）
SSE 連線每 IP 同時 5 條
POST /link-preview每 user 20 / 分鐘（P1）
HTTP/1.1 429 Too Many Requests
Content-Type: application/problem+json
Retry-After: 23
RateLimit-Policy: 30;w=60
RateLimit-Limit: 30
RateLimit-Remaining: 0
RateLimit-Reset: 23

{ "type": "https://api.wishsync.tw/problems/rate-limited", "title": "Too many requests", "status": 429,
  "detail": "請求過於頻繁，請 23 秒後再試。", "code": "RATE_LIMITED" }

錯誤格式（RFC 9457）

所有 4xx / 5xx 皆為 application/problem+json，固定欄位 type、title、status、detail、code，另有 instance、request_id，並可依錯誤附加欄位（errors[]、remaining、remaining_points）。前端以 code 做分支，detail 為可直接顯示的繁中訊息。

HTTP/1.1 409 Conflict
Content-Type: application/problem+json
X-Request-Id: 0192f5d0-1a2b-7c3d-8e4f-5a6b7c8d9e0f

{
  "type": "https://api.wishsync.tw/problems/item-fully-claimed",
  "title": "Item fully claimed",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "此品項剩餘 1 件，無法再認領 3 件。",
  "code": "ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED",
  "instance": "/api/v1/items/0192f3a1-0a11-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f01/claims",
  "request_id": "0192f5d0-1a2b-7c3d-8e4f-5a6b7c8d9e0f",
  "remaining": 1
}
HTTP/1.1 422 Unprocessable Content
Content-Type: application/problem+json

{
  "type": "https://api.wishsync.tw/problems/validation-failed",
  "title": "Validation failed",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "請求內容有 2 個欄位不正確。",
  "code": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "request_id": "0192f5d0-2b3c-7d4e-9f5a-6b7c8d9e0f1a",
  "errors": [
    { "pointer": "/qty", "code": "RANGE", "detail": "qty 必須介於 1 與 99" },
    { "pointer": "/display_name", "code": "REQUIRED", "detail": "首次認領需填寫暱稱" }
  ]
}

唯讀模式（READ_ONLY_MODE）

當 system_flags.read_only = true（DB 遷移、維護時啟用），所有非安全方法（POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE）一律回 503 READ_ONLY_MODE 並帶 Retry-After；GET / HEAD / OPTIONS 與 /internal/* 不受影響。前端遇到此碼應顯示「系統維護中，稍後再試」，並可沿用同一把 Idempotency-Key 重試。

CORS

僅允許 https://app.wishsync.tw（精確比對，不可用 *，因為要 credentials）。Nuxt SSR 由伺服器端呼叫公開 API 時不經 CORS，但需自行轉發 ws_session / X-Guest-Token（若需要）。

Access-Control-Allow-Origin: https://app.wishsync.tw
Access-Control-Allow-Credentials: true
Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, PATCH, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS
Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, Idempotency-Key, X-Guest-Token
Access-Control-Expose-Headers: Location, Retry-After, RateLimit-Limit, RateLimit-Remaining, RateLimit-Reset, RateLimit-Policy, X-Request-Id, Idempotency-Replayed
Access-Control-Max-Age: 600
Vary: Origin

分頁與快取

端點類型Cache-Control
GET /public/wishlists/{slug}public, s-maxage=10, stale-while-revalidate=60
GET /public/relief/*public, s-maxage=30, stale-while-revalidate=120
GET /config/public（P2）public, s-maxage=300, stale-while-revalidate=600
GET /public/gift-cards/{code}（P2-B）no-store（含個人化祝福語與防猜碼，不可被 CDN 快取）
GET /public/wishlists/{slug}/eventsno-cache, no-transform
所有需認證端點（含 /guest/me、/me）private, no-store

Auth 認證

方法路徑期認證
POST/auth/otp/requestP0公開
POST/auth/otp/verifyP0公開
GET/auth/oauth/{provider}/startP0公開
GET/auth/oauth/{provider}/callbackP0公開（需 ws_oauth_state）
POST/auth/logoutP0ws_session
GET/meP0ws_session

POST/api/v1/auth/otp/requestP0

認證：公開

寄送 Email OTP（6 位數，10 分鐘有效）。不論 email 是否已註冊都回 202，避免帳號列舉。

Request body
{ "email": "mei@example.com" }
Response 202
{ "expires_in": 600, "resend_after": 60 }
可能錯誤碼
VALIDATION_FAILED RATE_LIMITED
限流：每 email 5 次 / 小時、每 IP 20 次 / 小時。

POST/api/v1/auth/otp/verifyP0

認證：公開

驗證 OTP；成功即建立 user（若不存在）與 session，回應 Set-Cookie: ws_session。

Request body
{ "email": "mei@example.com", "code": "493027" }
Response 200
Set-Cookie: ws_session=<opaque>; Path=/; Max-Age=2592000; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax
{
  "user": { "id": "0192f3a0-1111-7000-8000-000000000001", "display_name": "mei", "email": "mei@example.com", "avatar_url": null },
  "is_new_user": true
}
可能錯誤碼
VALIDATION_FAILED OTP_INVALID RATE_LIMITED
OTP_INVALID 涵蓋錯誤 / 過期 / 已使用，不區分以免洩漏；連續錯 5 次作廢該挑戰。

GET/api/v1/auth/oauth/{provider}/startP0

認證：公開

開始 OAuth / OIDC 登入，302 導向 LINE 或 Google。

Path 參數
provider: line | google
Query 參數
redirect（選填）：登入後回到前端的相對路徑，預設 /dashboard；必須以 / 開頭，伺服器白名單驗證
Response 302
Set-Cookie: ws_oauth_state=<signed>; Path=/api/v1/auth/oauth; Max-Age=600; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax
（無 body）Location: https://access.line.me/oauth2/v2.1/authorize?...&state=...&code_challenge=...
可能錯誤碼
VALIDATION_FAILED RATE_LIMITED
使用 state + PKCE。ws_oauth_state 為補充 cookie，僅用於一次 OAuth 流程。

GET/api/v1/auth/oauth/{provider}/callbackP0

認證：公開（需 ws_oauth_state）

OAuth 回呼：換 token、綁定或建立 auth_identities，建立 session 後 302 回 https://app.wishsync.tw 。

Path 參數
provider: line | google
Query 參數
code, state（由供應商帶回）；error（使用者拒絕時）
Response 302
Set-Cookie: ws_session=<opaque>; Path=/; Max-Age=2592000; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax
（無 body）Location: https://app.wishsync.tw/dashboard
可能錯誤碼
OAUTH_STATE_INVALID OAUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR UNAUTHORIZED RATE_LIMITED
使用者拒絕或 state 失敗時 302 到 https://app.wishsync.tw/login?error=oauth_failed，不回 JSON；供應商端點故障（token 交換失敗 / 逾時）回 502 OAUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR。

POST/api/v1/auth/logoutP0

認證：ws_session

撤銷目前 session 並清除 cookie。

Response 204
Set-Cookie: ws_session=; Max-Age=0; Path=/; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax
（無 body）
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED

GET/api/v1/meP0

認證：ws_session

目前登入者資料與所屬組織。

Response 200
{
  "id": "0192f3a0-1111-7000-8000-000000000001",
  "display_name": "小米",
  "email": "mei@example.com",
  "avatar_url": null,
  "is_staff": false,
  "notification_prefs": { "email_claims": true },
  "identities": [ { "provider": "email" }, { "provider": "line" } ],
  "orgs": [ { "id": "0192f4c0-0a01-7d11-9a22-4c5d6e7f8a01", "name": "花蓮在地互助會", "role": "owner" } ]

可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED
is_staff = true 的使用者可進前端 /admin 營運後台（MVP 起）。通知設定用 PATCH /me 更新。

PATCH/api/v1/wishlists/{id}P0

認證：ws_session（擁有者）

部分更新。可更新 title、description、cover_image_key、event_date、visibility、status、show_claimer_names、surprise_mode、claim_ttl_hours。發佈沒有獨立端點：以 { "status": "active" } 發佈。

Request body
{
  "status": "active",
  "show_claimer_names": true
}
Response 200
（回傳完整 Wishlist，格式同 POST /wishlists 201）
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND VALIDATION_FAILED WISHLIST_NOT_PUBLISHABLE SHIPPING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED WISHLIST_CLOSED
發佈條件（draft → active）：title 非空；至少 1 個未刪除品項；每個 crowdfund 品項有 target_points、funding_deadline 與 fulfillment_type（P2）；surprise_mode 時 event_date 為今日之後。不符回 409 WISHLIST_NOT_PUBLISHABLE，errors[] 列出缺漏項目。含眾籌品項但尚未填收件地址（PUT /wishlists/{id}/shipping-address）→ 409 SHIPPING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED。status 允許 draft→active→closed→archived（closed 可重開為 active）。closed / archived 後修改品項回 WISHLIST_CLOSED。驚喜鎖定期間不可關閉 surprise_mode（回 FORBIDDEN）。每次成功更新會對 SSE 廣播 wishlist.updated。

DELETE/api/v1/wishlists/{id}P0

認證：ws_session（擁有者）

封存（D13）：將 status 設為 archived，不實體刪除。公開頁立即回 404；既有 claims 保留，訪客在 /me/claims 仍可看到（顯示「清單已封存」）；仍有 pledged 認捐時一併退回錢包（P2）。重複 DELETE 回 204（冪等）。

Response 204
（無 body）
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND

GET/api/v1/wishlists/{id}/dashboardP0

認證：ws_session（擁有者；relief 為組織成員）

完成度儀表板。驚喜鎖定期間（surprise_mode 且 now < unlock_at）只回整體彙總（completion_pct 等）：每品項的 qty_claimed / pledged_points 為 null；不含 claims、contributions、暱稱、聯絡方式、留言；眾籌只回採購單彙總 orders_summary（已達標 / 已下單 N / 運送中 M / 已送達），不提供逐品項對應，也不看捐贈者。出貨 / 送達通知只發給捐贈者（D22-11）。

Response 200
{
  "wishlist_id": "0192f3a0-7c1e-7b2a-9d4e-1a2b3c4d5e01",
  "surprise_locked": true,
  "unlock_at": "2026-11-30T16:00:00Z",
  "totals": {
    "item_count": 12, "fulfilled_count": 4, "completion_pct": 33,
    "qty_needed": 40, "qty_claimed": 14,

  "items": [
    { "item_id": "0192f3a1-0a11-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f01", "title": "玻璃奶瓶 240ml", "funding_mode": "quantity", "qty_needed": 10, "qty_claimed": null,
      "target_points": null, "pledged_points": null, "funding_status": null },
    { "item_id": "0192f3a1-0a12-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f02", "title": "嬰兒推車", "funding_mode": "crowdfund", "qty_needed": 1, "qty_claimed": null,
      "target_points": 12000, "pledged_points": null, "funding_status": "open" }
  ],
  "orders_summary": { "funded_count": 0, "ordered_count": 1, "shipped_count": 0, "delivered_count": 0 },
  "claims": null,
  "contributions": null
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND
解鎖後（或未開驚喜模式）claims 為 Claim[]，contributions 為 [{ id, item_id, points, message, status, display_name, created_at }]：捐贈者 is_anonymous=true 時 display_name 固定為「匿名朋友」（連解鎖後也不揭露），不提供捐贈者 email / 聯絡方式。unlock_at = event_date 當日 00:00 Asia/Taipei 換算 UTC。鎖定期間 totals 仍回傳整體彙總（完成度）。公開頁 /s/{slug} 仍顯示各品項 x/y（避免重複送禮所必需），前端偵測到瀏覽者是建立者本人時顯示「驚喜模式中」遮罩（軟性遮蔽；已知限制：建立者可用無痕視窗看到，接受）。眾籌品項的包裹送達本身即揭曉驚喜。

POST/api/v1/wishlists/{id}/itemsP0

  "expired_at": null,
  "order_status": null,
  "sort_order": 10,
  "created_at": "2026-10-08T03:15:00Z",
  "updated_at": "2026-10-08T03:15:00Z"
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND VALIDATION_FAILED CATALOG_PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE WISHLIST_CLOSED
MVP（P0/P1）只能建立 quantity 品項：funding_mode=crowdfund 在 P2 啟用前回 422 VALIDATION_FAILED（errors[].pointer=/funding_mode）。
P2 眾籌（點數）範例：目錄商品 { "title": "嬰兒推車", "funding_mode": "crowdfund", "fulfillment_type": "catalog", "catalog_product_id": "<uuid>" }，伺服器以目錄價 + 運費 + 緩衝計算 target_points 並寫入 price_snapshot_amount；人工代購 { "title": "…", "funding_mode": "crowdfund", "fulfillment_type": "concierge", "product_url": "https://…", "price_snapshot_amount": 8000 }，target_points = 商品價（含運）+ 服務費 + 緩衝（費率見 GET /config/public 的 concierge_fee_rate / concierge_buffer_rate；實際 target_points 以後端計算為準，前端僅預覽）。funding_deadline 可選，預設 event_date 23:59 Asia/Taipei。目錄商品下架 / 缺貨 → 409 CATALOG_PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE。relief 據點品項須帶 category 與 urgency，且只能 quantity（P2）。

PATCH/api/v1/items/{item_id}P0

認證：ws_session（擁有者；relief 為協調者以上）

部分更新品項。

Request body
{ "qty_needed": 12, "priority": "high" }
Response 200
（回傳完整 WishlistItem）
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND VALIDATION_FAILED QTY_BELOW_CLAIMED ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS WISHLIST_CLOSED
qty_needed 不可小於 qty_claimed（QTY_BELOW_CLAIMED）；已有認領 / 認捐後（pledged_points > 0）不可變更 funding_mode、fulfillment_type、catalog_product_id、target_points（ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS）；品項 funded 之後一律唯讀。成功後廣播 item.updated。

DELETE/api/v1/items/{item_id}P0

認證：ws_session（擁有者）

軟刪除品項。若有進行中的 claims / contributions，須帶 ?force=true：claims 一併 cancelled（並通知認領者，P1）、pledged 認捐一併 released（點數退回各捐贈者錢包，P2）；已 funded（captured）的品項不可刪除（ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS）。

Query 參數
force（選填，bool，預設 false）
Response 204
（無 body）
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED NOT_FOUND ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS

Uploads・Link preview

方法路徑期認證
POST/uploads/presignP0ws_session
POST/link-previewP1ws_session

POST/api/v1/uploads/presignP0

認證：ws_session

取得 R2 presigned PUT URL，前端直傳後把 object_key 填入 cover_image_key / image_key。

Request body
{ "purpose": "item", "content_type": "image/webp", "content_length": 183422 }
Response 200
{
  "upload_url": "https://<account>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com/wishsync-media/incoming/0192f3a1-bbbb?X-Amz-...",
  "method": "PUT",
  "headers": { "Content-Type": "image/webp", "Content-Length": "183422" },
  "object_key": "items/0192f3a1-bbbb.webp",
  "public_url": "https://img.wishsync.tw/items/0192f3a1-bbbb.webp",
  "processing": true,
  "expires_at": "2026-10-08T03:25:00Z"
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED VALIDATION_FAILED RATE_LIMITED
限制：purpose: cover | item；content_type 只允許 image/jpeg、image/png、image/webp，content_length 上限 5 MB（超出或型別不符 → 422，errors[].pointer 指出欄位）；presigned URL 5 分鐘有效，並把 Content-Type / Content-Length 簽進簽章。
EXIF 處理（D35）：原檔先上傳到私有的 incoming/ 前綴（不經 CDN、不公開）。上傳完成後由背景 job 驗證檔頭與大小、重新編碼並去除全部 EXIF／中繼資料（含 GPS 座標）、套用方向後寫入公開的 object_key（可用 Cloudflare Images 轉檔，或 job 自行重編碼），完成前 public_url 為 404，品項 image_status = pending 且 image_url 為 null（processing: true，通常數秒內完成），公開頁不顯示圖片；處理成功 → ready（才有 image_url），驗證失敗（偽造副檔名、超過大小、無法解碼）→ rejected，檔案刪除、image_key 清空，前端提示重新上傳。品項狀態 none | pending | ready | rejected（wishlist_items.image_status，D47-6；清單封面為 cover_image_status）。這是為了避免孕婦 / 家庭住址外洩；前端上傳頁也要提示「請勿上傳含個人資料的照片」。

POST/api/v1/link-previewP1

認證：ws_session

抓取商品網址的標題與圖片（需防 SSRF：僅 http/https、DNS 解析後拒絕私有 / loopback / link-local 位址、限制重導次數與回應大小、逾時 5 秒）。

Request body
{ "url": "https://example.com/p/123" }
Response 200
{
  "url": "https://example.com/p/123",
  "final_url": "https://example.com/p/123?ref=share",
  "title": "Pigeon 玻璃奶瓶 240ml",
  "image_url": "https://example.com/img/123.jpg",
  "site_name": "Example Shop",
  "price_amount": 450
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED VALIDATION_FAILED URL_BLOCKED UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT RATE_LIMITED
price_amount 為盡力解析（schema.org / OG），可為 null。image_url 為原站網址，使用者確認後前端再走 presign 上傳（或由後端轉存 R2，待決）。

Response 200

Content-Type: application/json

  "slug": "Ab3dE5gH7k",
  "type": "registry",
  "status": "active",
  "title": "小米與阿嘉的寶寶迎接清單",

  "owner": { "id": "0192f3a0-1111-7000-8000-000000000001", "display_name": "小米" },
  "surprise_mode": true,
  "claimers_visible": false,
  "completion": { "item_count": 2, "fulfilled_count": 0, "completion_pct": 52 },
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "0192f3a1-0a11-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f01",
      "title": "玻璃奶瓶 240ml",
      "brand": "Pigeon",
      "spec": "寬口徑 / 3 入裝",
      "image_url": "https://img.wishsync.tw/items/0192f3a1-bbbb.webp",
      "product_url": "https://example.com/p/123",
      "unit_price_amount": 450,
      "priority": "medium",
      "funding_mode": "quantity",
      "qty_needed": 10,
      "qty_claimed": 6,
      "qty_remaining": 4,
      "is_fully_claimed": false,
      "target_points": null,
      "pledged_points": null,
      "remaining_points": null,
      "funding_status": null,
      "display_status": null,
      "funding_deadline": null,
      "progress_percent": 60
    },
    {
      "id": "0192f3a1-0a12-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f02",
      "title": "嬰兒推車",
      "brand": "Combi",
      "spec": null,
      "image_url": "https://img.wishsync.tw/items/0192f3a1-cccc.webp",
      "product_url": null,
      "unit_price_amount": 12000,
      "priority": "high",
      "funding_mode": "crowdfund",
      "qty_needed": 1,
      "qty_claimed": 0,
      "qty_remaining": 1,
      "is_fully_claimed": false,
      "target_points": 12000,
      "pledged_points": 5000,
      "remaining_points": 7000,
      "funding_status": "open",
      "display_status": "open",
      "funding_deadline": "2026-12-01T15:59:00Z",
      "progress_percent": 41
    }
  ],
  "updated_at": "2026-10-08T05:10:00Z"
}
可能錯誤碼
NOT_FOUND WISHLIST_REMOVED RATE_LIMITED
遮蔽規則（上方範例為 surprise_mode=true 且 show_claimer_names=false）：
  • items[] 只有 qty_claimed / qty_needed / qty_remaining 等彙總，沒有 claims，沒有 claimers 欄位；claimers_visible=false。
  • show_claimer_names=true 且 非驚喜鎖定期間，每個 item 才多出 "claimers": [{ "display_name": "小明", "qty": 3 }]，claimers_visible=true。驚喜鎖定期間無論 show_claimer_names 為何一律不輸出（伺服器端強制）。
  • 眾籌（P2）：公開 target_points / pledged_points / remaining_points / funding_status / display_status / funding_deadline。display_status（D22-6）是由 funding_status 與採購單狀態推導的衍生欄位，前端徽章直接使用：open（募集中）、funded（已達標，排隊下單）、ordered（已下單）、shipped（運送中）、delivered（已送達；fulfilled 亦對應此值）、expired（已截止未達標）；quantity 品項為 null；is_anonymous=true 的捐贈者永遠顯示為「匿名朋友」，非匿名者只在 show_claimer_names=true 且非驚喜鎖定期間才以 contributors: [{ "display_name": "小明", "points": 3000 }] 出現。P0/P1 的品項一律為 quantity，眾籌欄位皆為 null。
  • 回應含 surprise_mode 與 owner.id，前端比對 GET /me 的 id，若為建立者本人且 surprise_mode=true 則顯示「驚喜模式中」遮罩（軟性遮蔽，見 D4）。
  • moderation_status=hidden（被檢舉下架）→ 410 WISHLIST_REMOVED（前端顯示「此清單已被下架」，body 不含下架原因）。
  • status=draft / archived、visibility=private、已刪除 → 404。status=closed → 200（前端顯示已結束，認領 API 回 WISHLIST_CLOSED）。
  • relief（visibility=public）額外回傳 site: { location: {lat, lng}, address, site_status, org: {id, name} }，items 含 category、urgency、qty_received。
快取：Cache-Control: public, s-maxage=10, stale-while-revalidate=60（Cloudflare 邊緣快取 10 秒，過期後 60 秒內可先回舊資料並背景重新驗證）；瀏覽器端不快取（不設 max-age）。回應不得帶 Set-Cookie，且 Cloudflare cache key 忽略 cookie。SSE 推播負責讓開啟中的頁面即時更新，所以短 TTL 可接受。支援 ETag / If-None-Match → 304。

GET/api/v1/public/wishlists/{slug}/eventsP0

認證：公開

Server-Sent Events 串流。僅推送與公開頁相同遮蔽等級的資料。

Path 參數
slug
Response 200
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
Content-Type: text/event-stream
Cache-Control: no-cache, no-transform
X-Accel-Buffering: no

retry: 5000

id: 1760000123-1
event: item.updated
data: {"item_id":"0192f3a1-0a11-7c3b-8e5f-2b3c4d5e6f01","qty_needed":10,"qty_claimed":7,"qty_remaining":3,"is_fully_claimed":false,"pledged_points":null,"target_points":null,"funding_status":null,"display_status":null,"updated_at":"2026-10-08T05:12:00Z"}
