PATCH/api/v1/admin/gift-card-batches/{id}P2P2-C

認證：ws_session 且 users.is_staff = true

推進批次狀態：draft → invoiced → paid → issued；或 void。

Request body
{ "status": "invoiced", "invoice_no": "AB-12345678" }
Response 200
{
  "id": "0192f6a0-0f01-7f01-8b01-6a7b8c9d0e06", "corporate_account_id": "0192f6a0-2001-7f01-8b01-6a7b8c9d0e20",
  "quantity": 100, "face_value_points": 1000, "total_amount": 100000,
  "status": "invoiced", "invoice_no": "AB-12345678", "paid_at": null, "issued_at": null, "codes_exported": false,
  "created_at": "2026-10-08T06:05:00Z"
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED STAFF_ONLY NOT_FOUND VALIDATION_FAILED INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION
  • invoiced：必填 invoice_no（三聯式含統編；開立時點待會計師確認），伺服器建立 invoices 列（source_type=gift_card_batch、carrier_type=b2b、buyer_tax_id 取自企業統編）並寫入 gift_card_batches.invoice_id。
  • paid：營運確認入帳（銀行轉帳 / 匯款）後標記，記錄 paid_at。
  • issued：僅 paid 可轉；系統一次產生 quantity 張 gift_cards(batch_id)（status=active，同 D25 規則：兌換後 cash_refundable=false、不到期、只能用於心願品項），code 僅存 hash，暫存密文供匯出一次。
  • void：未發卡前作廢；已發卡後整批退款依合約，未兌換卡轉 void，已兌換者不受影響。其餘轉換 → 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION。

GET/api/v1/admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/codesP2P2-C

認證：ws_session 且 users.is_staff = true

匯出兌換連結 CSV（交給企業，或由系統依企業提供的 email 清單寄送）。只能匯出一次：匯出後立即清除暫存密文。

Response 200
HTTP/1.1 200 OK
Content-Type: text/csv; charset=utf-8
Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="batch-0192f6a0-0f01.csv"
Cache-Control: no-store

card_id,code,share_url
0192f6a0-3001-7f01-8b01-6a7b8c9d0e31,7K2QMXA4D9R5T8VB,https://app.wishsync.tw/g/7K2QMXA4D9R5T8VB
0192f6a0-3002-7f01-8b01-6a7b8c9d0e32,B8N3WQ5F2H7J9K4M,https://app.wishsync.tw/g/B8N3WQ5F2H7J9K4M
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED STAFF_ONLY NOT_FOUND INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED
批次不是 issued → 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION；已匯出過（gift_card_batches.codes_exported_at 非 NULL）→ 409 CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED，CSV 遺失請用 POST /admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/reissue 補發。匯出後所有卡的 code_enc 即清為 NULL（D31-1）。匯出行為寫入 audit_logs（giftbatch.export_codes）。

POST/api/v1/admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/reissueP2P2-C

認證：ws_session 且 users.is_staff = true

補發（D31-8）：CSV 遺失時，該批次所有 active 未兌換卡 void，產生同數量新卡，codes_exported_at 重置為 NULL，可再匯出一次。已兌換的卡不受影響。

Response 200
{{
  "batch_id": "0192f6a0-0f01-7f01-8b01-6a7b8c9d0e06",
  "voided_cards": 100,
  "issued_cards": 100,
  "codes_exported": false
}}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED STAFF_ONLY NOT_FOUND INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION
僅 issued 批次可補發（否則 409 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION）；整個動作在單一交易內（4.3r），並寫 audit_logs（giftbatch.reissue）。舊連結立即失效（GIFT_CARD_INVALID）。

內部端點（不屬公開 API）

方法路徑期認證
POST/internal/jobs/tickP0Cloud Scheduler OIDC token（服務帳號；不經 Cloudflare，不在公開 API 與 OpenAPI）

POST/internal/jobs/tickP0

認證：Cloud Scheduler OIDC token（服務帳號；不經 Cloudflare，不在公開 API 與 OpenAPI）

背景工作觸發點（D8）。Cloud Scheduler 每分鐘 POST 一次；每次執行下列到期工作並回報結果。各工作皆為冪等，可重複觸發。

Request headers
Authorization: Bearer <Google OIDC ID token>（audience = 服務 URL；驗證 email 為排程專用服務帳號）
Response 200
{
  "ran_at": "2026-10-08T05:01:00Z",
  "jobs": [
    { "name": "claims.expire", "affected": 2 },
    { "name": "lots.expire_bonus", "affected": 4 },
    { "name": "rewards.stars_expire_and_tier", "affected": 12 },
    { "name": "wallet.auto_topup", "affected": 1 },
    { "name": "funding.expire", "affected": 1 },
    { "name": "funding.release_after_window", "affected": 0 },
    { "name": "outbox.dispatch", "affected": 3 },
    { "name": "idempotency.purge", "affected": 14 },
    { "name": "otp_sessions.purge", "affected": 3 },
    { "name": "notifications.dispatch", "affected": 0 }
  ]
}
可能錯誤碼
UNAUTHORIZED FORBIDDEN INTERNAL_ERROR
  • claims.expire：reserved 且 expires_at 已過 → expired 並回補 qty_claimed（第 4 章 4.2）。
  • lots.expire_bonus（P2-B，每日）：到期的 reward_bonus 批次餘額歸零、扣 wallet.balance、ledger expire（4.3o）。rewards.stars_expire_and_tier（P2-B，每日）：星星到期與等級評估（4.3n）。rewards.earn / rewards.clawback（outbox）：認捐 captured 後賺星、購買失敗扣回。wallet.auto_topup（P2-A，outbox）：自動儲值扣卡，含當月上限檢查（4.3p）。giftcard.refund（P2-B，outbox）：未兌換禮物卡原路退款。
  • funding.expire（P2）：眾籌品項 funding_deadline 已過且仍 open → expired，並通知捐贈者（4.3d）。
  • funding.release_after_window（P2）：expired 滿 7 天仍 pledged 的認捐自動退回錢包（4.3d）。
  • outbox.dispatch（P2）：處理 outbox_events（order.place 目錄商品呼叫商家 API 下單；concierge 進營運佇列；失敗 / 截止通知），以 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED 領取。
  • idempotency.purge、otp_sessions.purge：清除過期資料（4.8）。
  • notifications.dispatch（P1）：發送到期提醒與通知佇列。
只接受來自 Cloud Scheduler 的 OIDC 呼叫，無有效 token 回 401，非排程服務帳號回 403；此路徑不套用 CORS、不對外公布，也不受唯讀模式（READ_ONLY_MODE）阻擋。多實例同時收到時以 SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED 或 advisory lock 避免重複執行。

6. TypeScript 型別定義

給 Nuxt 前端使用，與 JSON 一一對應；enum 一律 string union。建議由 OpenAPI 產生（如 openapi-typescript），此處為手寫契約基準。

// ===== enums（string union，與 PostgreSQL enum 一一對應）=====
export type WishlistType = 'personal' | 'registry' | 'relief'
export type WishlistStatus = 'draft' | 'active' | 'closed' | 'archived'
export type Visibility = 'link' | 'private' | 'public'
export type FundingMode = 'quantity' | 'crowdfund'
export type ItemPriority = 'high' | 'medium' | 'low'
export type ReliefUrgency = 'critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low'
export type ReliefCategory = 'water' | 'food' | 'medical' | 'hygiene' | 'shelter' | 'clothing' | 'baby' | 'other'
export type ClaimStatus = 'reserved' | 'purchased' | 'delivered' | 'cancelled' | 'expired'
export type ContributionStatus = 'pledged' | 'captured' | 'released' | 'reallocated'
export type FundingStatus = 'open' | 'funded' | 'expired' | 'fulfilled'
export type FulfillmentType = 'catalog' | 'concierge'
export type LedgerEntryType = 'topup' | 'pledge' | 'release' | 'cash_refund' | 'adjustment' | 'gift_claim' | 'reward_bonus' | 'expire'
export type PointLotSource = 'topup' | 'gift_card' | 'reward_bonus' | 'adjustment'
export type GiftCardStatus = 'active' | 'claimed' | 'refunded' | 'void'
export type GiftOrderStatus = 'pending' | 'paid' | 'failed' | 'refunded'
export type GiftBatchStatus = 'draft' | 'invoiced' | 'paid' | 'issued' | 'void'
export type StarReason = 'earn' | 'redeem' | 'clawback' | 'expire'
export type RewardTier = 'green' | 'gold'
export type TopupStatus = 'pending' | 'paid' | 'failed' | 'refunded' | 'disputed' | 'charged_back'
export type ModerationStatus = 'ok' | 'hidden'
export type ReportReason = 'scam' | 'inappropriate' | 'copyright' | 'personal_info' | 'other'
export type ReportStatus = 'open' | 'actioned' | 'dismissed'
export type WalletStatus = 'active' | 'frozen'
export type InvoiceStatus = 'pending' | 'issued' | 'void' | 'allowance'
export type InvoiceCarrierType = 'member' | 'mobile_barcode' | 'citizen_cert' | 'donate' | 'b2b'
export type VerificationStatus = 'pending' | 'verified' | 'rejected'
export type ImageStatus = 'none' | 'pending' | 'ready' | 'rejected'
export type WalletDebtStatus = 'open' | 'settled' | 'written_off'
export type RefundRequestStatus = 'requested' | 'processing' | 'completed' | 'rejected'
export type OrderStatus = 'pending' | 'placed' | 'shipped' | 'delivered' | 'failed' | 'cancelled'
export type PaymentProvider = 'tappay' | 'ecpay'          // 由後端依 method 決定，前端不傳
export type PaymentMethod = 'card' | 'line_pay' | 'apple_pay'
export type DisplayStatus = 'open' | 'funded' | 'ordered' | 'shipped' | 'delivered' | 'expired'   // 前端眾籌徽章直接用
export type OrgRole = 'owner' | 'coordinator' | 'volunteer'
export type SiteStatus = 'active' | 'paused' | 'closed'
export type AuthProvider = 'email' | 'line' | 'google'

// ===== 通用 =====
export type UUID = string        // UUID v7
export type ISODateTime = string // 2026-10-08T03:12:45Z（UTC）
export type ISODate = string     // 2026-12-01
export type Amount = number      // 新台幣元，整數（catalog price_amount / shipping_fee_amount 等）
export type Points = number      // 點數，整數；1 點 = NT$1

export interface Paginated<T> { data: T[]; next_cursor: string | null }
export interface LatLng { lat: number; lng: number }

export interface ProblemDetails {
  type: string                 // https://api.wishsync.tw/problems/<kebab-code>
  title: string
  status: number
  detail?: string
  code: ErrorCode
  instance?: string
  request_id?: string
  errors?: { pointer: string; code: string; detail: string }[]  // VALIDATION_FAILED
  remaining?: number           // ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED
  remaining_points?: Points    // CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED（硬上限）
  claimable_at?: ISODateTime   // GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE
  limit_amount?: Amount        // TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED
  remaining_amount?: Amount    // TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED
  balance?: Points             // INSUFFICIENT_POINTS
}

export type ErrorCode =
  | 'BAD_REQUEST' | 'VALIDATION_FAILED' | 'UNAUTHORIZED' | 'FORBIDDEN' | 'NOT_FOUND' | 'RATE_LIMITED'
  | 'IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED' | 'IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT' | 'WISHLIST_CLOSED'
  | 'ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED' | 'FUNDING_MODE_MISMATCH' | 'CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED'
  | 'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION' | 'QTY_BELOW_CLAIMED' | 'ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS'
  | 'CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS' | 'WISHLIST_NOT_PUBLISHABLE' | 'SITE_NOT_ACTIVE'
  | 'INSUFFICIENT_POINTS' | 'ITEM_FUNDED' | 'CONTRIBUTION_LOCKED' | 'REALLOCATION_NOT_ALLOWED'
  | 'FUNDING_EXPIRED' | 'GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE' | 'TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED' | 'WISHLIST_REMOVED' | 'ACCOUNT_HAS_BALANCE' | 'WALLET_FROZEN' | 'ORG_NOT_VERIFIED' | 'GIFT_CARD_INVALID' | 'GIFT_CARD_ALREADY_CLAIMED' | 'GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE' | 'NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE'
  | 'INSUFFICIENT_STARS' | 'AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED' | 'CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED' | 'SHIPPING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED' | 'CATALOG_PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE' | 'PAYMENT_FAILED' | 'STAFF_ONLY'
  | 'USER_NOT_FOUND' | 'MEMBER_ALREADY_EXISTS' | 'LAST_OWNER' | 'OAUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR' | 'READ_ONLY_MODE'
  | 'OTP_INVALID' | 'OAUTH_STATE_INVALID' | 'URL_BLOCKED'
  | 'INTERNAL_ERROR' | 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE' | 'UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT'

// ===== 建立者視角 =====
export interface Wishlist {
  id: UUID
  type: WishlistType
  status: WishlistStatus
  visibility: Visibility
  slug: string
  title: string
  description: string | null
  cover_image_url: string | null
  event_date: ISODate | null
  show_claimer_names: boolean
  surprise_mode: boolean
  surprise_locked: boolean          // 衍生欄位：surprise_mode 且 now < unlock_at
  claim_ttl_hours: number | null    // null = 認領不逾期；relief 預設 48
  moderation_status: ModerationStatus   // hidden：公開頁 410；建立者在 dashboard 看到下架原因
  moderation_reason: string | null
  moderated_at: ISODateTime | null
  cover_image_status: ImageStatus
  has_shipping_address: boolean     // P2 眾籌：是否已填收件地址（不回傳明文）
  org_id: UUID | null
  location: LatLng | null
  address: string | null
  site_status: SiteStatus | null
  share_url: string
  created_at: ISODateTime
  updated_at: ISODateTime
}

export interface WishlistItem {
  id: UUID
  wishlist_id: UUID
  title: string
  description: string | null
  brand: string | null
  spec: string | null
  image_url: string | null          // image_status 非 ready 時為 null
  image_status: ImageStatus         // none | pending（EXIF 處理中）| ready | rejected
  product_url: string | null
  unit_price_amount: Amount | null
  funding_mode: FundingMode
  priority: ItemPriority
  category: ReliefCategory | null
  urgency: ReliefUrgency | null
  qty_needed: number
  qty_claimed: number | null        // 驚喜鎖定期間為 null
  qty_received: number
  // ---- crowdfund（P2）；quantity 品項皆為 null ----
  target_points: Points | null
  pledged_points: Points | null     // 驚喜鎖定期間為 null
  funding_status: FundingStatus | null
  funding_deadline: ISODateTime | null
  fulfillment_type: FulfillmentType | null
  catalog_product_id: UUID | null
  price_snapshot_amount: Amount | null
  expired_at: ISODateTime | null    // 轉為 expired 的時間；選擇期 = expired_at + 7 天
  order_status: OrderStatus | null  // 採購單狀態；驚喜鎖定期間為 null（僅 dashboard 提供彙總）
  sort_order: number
  created_at: ISODateTime
  updated_at: ISODateTime
}

export interface Claim {
  id: UUID
  item_id: UUID
  qty: number
  status: ClaimStatus
  claimer_name: string
  note: string | null
  expires_at: ISODateTime | null    // claim_ttl_hours 計算；purchased 後為 null
  created_at: ISODateTime
  updated_at: ISODateTime
  purchased_at: ISODateTime | null
  delivered_at: ISODateTime | null
  cancelled_at: ISODateTime | null
}

export interface Contribution {
  id: UUID
  item_id: UUID
  wishlist_id: UUID
  points: Points
  refunded_points: Points           // 已退回錢包（差額 / 失敗）；實際花費 = points - refunded_points
  status: ContributionStatus
  message: string | null
  is_anonymous: boolean
  reallocated_to_item_id: UUID | null
  captured_at: ISODateTime | null
  released_at: ISODateTime | null
  created_at: ISODateTime
  updated_at: ISODateTime
}

// ===== 點數錢包（P2）=====
export interface PointLot {
  id: UUID
  source: PointLotSource
  remaining_points: Points
  cash_refundable: boolean          // topup 為 true；gift_card / reward_bonus 為 false
  expires_at: ISODateTime | null    // 僅 reward_bonus（90 天）
}
export interface Wallet {
  id: UUID
  status: WalletStatus              // frozen：認捐 / 兌換 / 現金退款 → 403 WALLET_FROZEN
  balance: Points                   // = Σ lots.remaining_points
  held_points: Points               // pledged + captured 的實際佔用（不在 balance 內）
  refundable_points: Points         // 可申請退現金（topup 批次）
  gift_points: Points               // 禮物卡兌入，不可退現金
  bonus_points: Points              // 星星兌換的贊助點數（未到期）
  bonus_expiring_soon: Points       // 14 天內到期的 bonus
  bonus_next_expiry: ISODateTime | null
  lots: PointLot[]                  // 扣點順序：reward_bonus（最早到期）→ gift_card → topup
}
export interface LedgerEntry {
  id: UUID
  delta: Points                     // 正負
  balance_after: Points
  lot_id: UUID | null               // 異動所屬批次
  entry_type: LedgerEntryType
  ref_type: 'topup' | 'contribution' | 'purchase_order' | 'cash_refund_request' | 'gift_card' | 'reward_offer' | 'point_lot' | 'manual' | null
  ref_id: UUID | null
  created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface Topup { id: UUID; amount: Amount; method: PaymentMethod; provider: PaymentProvider; status: TopupStatus; paid_at: ISODateTime | null; created_at: ISODateTime }
export interface CashRefundRequest { id: UUID; amount: Amount; status: RefundRequestStatus; created_at: ISODateTime; completed_at: ISODateTime | null }

/** GET /wallet/contributions 的一列 */
export interface WalletContribution {
  contribution: Contribution
  item: { id: UUID; title: string; image_url: string | null; funding_status: FundingStatus; display_status: DisplayStatus
          funding_deadline: ISODateTime; reallocation_deadline: ISODateTime | null   // expired 時 = expired_at + 7 天
          target_points: Points; pledged_points: Points }
  wishlist: { slug: string; title: string; event_date: ISODate | null }
  can_withdraw: boolean; can_reallocate: boolean
}

// ===== 自動儲值（P2-A，D24）=====
export interface AutoTopup {
  enabled: boolean; threshold_points: Points; amount: Amount; monthly_cap_amount: Amount
  method: 'card'; card_last4: string | null          // 不回傳 card token
  used_this_month: Amount; limit_reached: boolean
}

// ===== 電子禮物卡（P2-B，D25）=====
// 面額與卡面主題由 GET /config/public 提供（gift_card_face_values / gift_card_themes），不要寫死
export interface GiftCard {                          // 購買者視角（GET /gift-cards/purchased）
  id: UUID; order_id: UUID; face_value_points: Points; theme: string; message: string | null
  sender_display_name: string | null; status: GiftCardStatus
  claimed_at: ISODateTime | null; refundable: boolean
  share_url?: string                                 // active 的卡才有（由 code_enc 解密）；claimed / refunded / void 後無法再取得
}
export interface PublicGiftCard {                    // GET /public/gift-cards/{code}
  face_value_points: Points; theme: string; message: string | null
  sender_display_name: string | null; claimable: boolean
}
export interface GiftCardOrder {
  id: UUID; quantity: number; face_value_points: Points; total_amount: Amount
  method: PaymentMethod; status: GiftOrderStatus; paid_at: ISODateTime | null; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface CreateGiftCardOrderRequest {
  face_value_points: Points; quantity: number          // 面額須在 config.gift_card_face_values；1 至 config.gift_card_max_quantity
  method: PaymentMethod; prime?: string; invoice?: InvoiceOptions
  theme?: string                                       // config.gift_card_themes[].id
  message?: string; sender_display_name?: string       // 祝福語以「訂單」為單位，同一訂單所有卡相同
}
export interface GiftCardOrderDetail {                // GET /gift-cards/orders/{id}（付款後前端輪詢）
  order: GiftCardOrder
  cards: GiftCard[] | null                           // status=paid 後才有，含 share_url
}
export interface ClaimGiftCardRequest { code: string; turnstile_token: string }

// ===== 回饋星星（P2-B，D26）=====
export interface RewardAccount {
  stars_balance: number; tier: RewardTier; tier_evaluated_at: ISODateTime | null
  stars_expiring_soon: { stars: number; expires_at: ISODateTime } | null
  gold_progress: { earned_12m: number; required: number }
}
export interface StarLedgerEntry {
  id: UUID; delta: number; reason: StarReason
  ref_type: 'contribution' | 'reward_offer' | 'purchase_order' | 'manual' | null; ref_id: UUID | null
  expires_at: ISODateTime | null; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface RewardOffer { id: UUID; stars_cost: number; bonus_points: Points; tier_required: RewardTier }

// ===== 企業禮券（P2-C，D27；STAFF_ONLY）=====
export interface CorporateAccount {
  id: UUID; name: string; tax_id: string; contact_name: string; contact_email: string
  billing_address: string; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface GiftCardBatch {
  id: UUID; corporate_account_id: UUID; quantity: number; face_value_points: Points; total_amount: Amount
  status: GiftBatchStatus; invoice_no: string | null; paid_at: ISODateTime | null
  issued_at: ISODateTime | null; codes_exported: boolean; created_at: ISODateTime
}

/** GET /config/public（P2；可 CDN 快取） */
export interface PublicConfig {
  concierge_fee_rate: number; concierge_buffer_rate: number
  gift_card_face_values: Amount[]; gift_card_max_quantity: number
  new_account_daily_limit_amount: Amount   // 新帳號（30 天內）每日儲值 + 禮物卡購買上限
  gift_card_themes: { id: string; name: string; image_url: string }[]
  topup_min_amount: Amount; topup_max_amount: Amount; topup_presets: Amount[]
  concierge_sla_business_days: number; reallocation_window_days: number
}

export interface PurchaseOrder {
  id: UUID
  item_id: UUID
  fulfillment_type: FulfillmentType
  status: OrderStatus
  tracking_no: string | null        // 驚喜鎖定期間為 null
  updated_at: ISODateTime
}

// ===== 公開頁（訪客視角；永不含認領者資料）=====
export interface PublicItem {
  id: UUID
  title: string
  brand: string | null
  spec: string | null
  image_url: string | null
  product_url: string | null
  unit_price_amount: Amount | null
  priority: ItemPriority
  funding_mode: FundingMode
  category?: ReliefCategory           // relief
  urgency?: ReliefUrgency             // relief
  qty_needed: number
  qty_claimed: number
  qty_remaining: number
  qty_received?: number               // relief
  is_fully_claimed: boolean
  target_points: Points | null        // crowdfund（P2）
  pledged_points: Points | null
  remaining_points: Points | null
  funding_status: FundingStatus | null
  display_status: DisplayStatus | null   // crowdfund 才有；前端徽章用
  funding_deadline: ISODateTime | null
  progress_percent: number
  /** 僅 claimers_visible=true 時出現（quantity） */
  claimers?: { display_name: string; qty: number }[]
  /** 僅 claimers_visible=true 時出現（crowdfund）；is_anonymous 的捐贈者顯示為「匿名朋友」 */
  contributors?: { display_name: string; points: Points }[]
}

export interface PublicWishlist {
  slug: string
  type: WishlistType
  status: WishlistStatus
  title: string
  description: string | null
  cover_image_url: string | null
  event_date: ISODate | null
  id: UUID
  owner: { id: UUID; display_name: string }   // 前端比對 /me 的 id，是建立者本人時顯示「驚喜模式中」遮罩
  surprise_mode: boolean
  claimers_visible: boolean
  completion: { item_count: number; fulfilled_count: number; completion_pct: number }
  items: PublicItem[]
  site?: { location: LatLng; address: string | null; site_status: SiteStatus; org: { id: UUID; name: string } } // relief
  updated_at: ISODateTime
}

// ===== request / response 範例 =====
export interface CreateClaimRequest {
  qty: number; display_name?: string; contact?: string; note?: string
  email?: string          // 選填：認領確認信與「管理我的認領」連結（D39）
}

// ===== 帳號 / 通知 / 營運後台（MVP）=====
export interface NotificationPrefs { email_claims: boolean }      // MVP 鍵（D47-5）；PATCH /me 更新
export interface Me {
  id: UUID; display_name: string; email: string | null; avatar_url: string | null
  is_staff: boolean; notification_prefs: NotificationPrefs
  identities: { provider: AuthProvider }[]
  orgs: { id: UUID; name: string; role: OrgRole }[]
}
export interface UpdateMeRequest { display_name?: string; notification_prefs?: Partial<NotificationPrefs> }
export interface DeleteMeRequest { confirm: 'DELETE' }          // 二次確認
export interface CreateReportRequest {
  reason: ReportReason; detail?: string; item_id?: UUID; turnstile_token: string
}
export interface ContentReport {
  id: UUID; wishlist: { id: UUID; slug: string; title: string }; item_id: UUID | null
  reason: ReportReason; detail: string | null; status: ReportStatus
  reporter: 'guest' | 'user' | 'anonymous'; handled_at: ISODateTime | null; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface ModerateWishlistRequest { moderation_status: ModerationStatus; reason?: string }   // hidden 時 reason 必填
export interface AdminWishlistRow {
  id: UUID; slug: string; title: string; status: WishlistStatus; type: WishlistType
  owner: { id: UUID; display_name: string; email: string | null }
  moderation_status: ModerationStatus; open_report_count: number; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface AdminUserRow {
  id: UUID; display_name: string; email: string | null; is_staff: boolean
  blocked: boolean; deleted: boolean; wishlist_count: number; created_at: ISODateTime
}
export interface SystemFlag { key: string; value: unknown; updated_at: ISODateTime }
export interface RecoverGuestRequest { token: string }              // POST /guest/recover：信件連結 /me/claims#r={token}
export interface RecoverGuestResponse { guest: { display_name: string }; guest_token: string }   // 同時 Set-Cookie ws_guest
export interface AdminWalletDebt {
  id: UUID; amount: Points; status: WalletDebtStatus; topup_id: UUID
  user: { id: UUID; display_name: string; email: string | null }; created_at: ISODateTime
  note: string | null; resolved_at: ISODateTime | null
}
export interface UpdateWalletStatusRequest { status: WalletStatus; reason: string }
export interface ResolveWalletDebtRequest { status: 'settled' | 'written_off'; note: string }

// ===== 電子發票選項（P2-A，D42）=====
export interface InvoiceOptions {
  carrier_type: InvoiceCarrierType            // 預設 member（會員載具）
  carrier_id?: string                         // mobile_barcode：/ABC1234；citizen_cert：自然人憑證
  donate_code?: string                        // donate：愛心碼
  buyer_tax_id?: string; buyer_name?: string  // b2b：公司統編（8 碼）
}

// ===== 救災組織驗證（P2-D，D43）=====
export interface OrgVerification { status: VerificationStatus; verified_at: ISODateTime | null; reason: string | null }
export interface CreateClaimResponse {
  claim: Claim
  item: { id: UUID; qty_needed: number; qty_claimed: number; qty_remaining: number }
  guest_token?: string                // 僅首次建立 guest 時出現
}
export interface CreateContributionRequest { points: Points; message?: string; is_anonymous?: boolean }
export interface CreateContributionResponse {
  contribution: Contribution
  item: { id: UUID; target_points: Points; pledged_points: Points; remaining_points: Points; funding_status: FundingStatus; progress_percent: number }
  wallet: { balance: Points }
  deducted_from: { source: PointLotSource; points: Points }[]   // 扣點來源批次（bonus → gift → topup）
  funded: boolean                     // 本次認捐是否剛好達標（已捕獲並建立採購單）
}
export interface CreateTopupRequest { amount: Amount; method: PaymentMethod; prime?: string; invoice?: InvoiceOptions }   // prime：經 TapPay 時必填（card / apple_pay / line_pay 皆由 TapPay SDK 取得）
export interface CreateTopupResponse {
  topup: Topup
  payment: { type: 'none' } | { type: 'redirect'; url: string } | { type: 'form_post'; url: string; fields: Record<string, string> }
}

// ===== SSE =====
export interface ItemUpdatedEvent {
  item_id: UUID; qty_needed: number; qty_claimed: number; qty_remaining: number
  is_fully_claimed: boolean; pledged_points: Points | null; target_points: Points | null
  funding_status: FundingStatus | null; display_status: DisplayStatus | null
  updated_at: ISODateTime; deleted?: boolean
}
export interface WishlistUpdatedEvent {
  status: WishlistStatus; title: string
  completion: PublicWishlist['completion']; updated_at: ISODateTime
}

// ===== fetch 範例：帶 Idempotency-Key 與 guest token =====
export async function createClaim(itemId: UUID, body: CreateClaimRequest, guestToken?: string): Promise<CreateClaimResponse> {
  const res = await fetch(`https://api.wishsync.tw/api/v1/items/${itemId}/claims`, {
    method: 'POST',
    credentials: 'include',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Idempotency-Key': crypto.randomUUID(),     // 重試同一次操作時必須沿用同一把 key
      ...(guestToken ? { 'X-Guest-Token': guestToken } : {}),
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  })
  if (!res.ok) throw (await res.json()) as ProblemDetails
  return res.json()
}

7. Rust 型別草案

代表性片段：sqlx 枚舉（type_name 對應 CREATE TYPE）、請求 / 回應 DTO、防超額認領 handler 骨架、AppError → problem+json。

use axum::{http::{header, HeaderValue, StatusCode}, response::{IntoResponse, Response}, Json};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use time::OffsetDateTime;
use uuid::Uuid;
use validator::Validate;

// ---------- sqlx::Type 枚舉：type_name 必須與 CREATE TYPE 同名 ----------
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::Type, utoipa::ToSchema)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "wishlist_type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum WishlistType { Personal, Registry, Relief }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::Type, utoipa::ToSchema)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "claim_status", rename_all = "snake_case")]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ClaimStatus { Reserved, Purchased, Delivered, Cancelled, Expired }

impl ClaimStatus {
    /// 狀態機：由應用層檢查，SQL 的 WHERE status IN (...) 為第二道防線
    pub fn can_transition_to(self, next: ClaimStatus) -> bool {
        use ClaimStatus::*;
        matches!((self, next),
            (Reserved, Purchased) | (Purchased, Delivered) | (Reserved, Delivered)
            | (Reserved, Cancelled) | (Purchased, Cancelled) | (Reserved, Expired))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, sqlx::FromRow, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct ContributionDto {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub item_id: Uuid,
    pub wishlist_id: Uuid,
    pub points: i64,
    pub refunded_points: i64,
    pub status: ContributionStatus,
    pub message: Option<String>,
    pub is_anonymous: bool,
    pub reallocated_to_item_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::Type, utoipa::ToSchema)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "contribution_status", rename_all = "snake_case")]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ContributionStatus { Pledged, Captured, Released, Reallocated }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::Type, utoipa::ToSchema)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "point_lot_source", rename_all = "snake_case")]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum PointLotSource { Topup, GiftCard, RewardBonus, Adjustment }

impl PointLotSource {
    /// 認捐扣點優先序：reward_bonus（最早到期）→ gift_card → topup；SQL 以相同 CASE 排序，這裡供單元測試與 UI 說明共用
    pub fn deduct_priority(self) -> u8 {
        match self { Self::RewardBonus => 0, Self::GiftCard => 1, Self::Topup => 2, Self::Adjustment => 3 }
    }
    pub fn cash_refundable(self) -> bool { matches!(self, Self::Topup) }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, sqlx::Type, utoipa::ToSchema)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "funding_status", rename_all = "snake_case")]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum FundingStatus { Open, Funded, Expired, Fulfilled }

/// POST /items/{item_id}/contributions（P2）：點數為整數，1 點 = NT$1
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Validate, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct CreateContributionRequest {
    #[validate(range(min = 1, max = 1_000_000))]
    pub points: i64,
    #[validate(length(max = 200))]
    pub message: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub is_anonymous: bool,
}

// ---------- request / response ----------
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Validate, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct CreateClaimRequest {
    #[validate(range(min = 1, max = 99))]
    pub qty: i32,
    #[validate(length(min = 1, max = 30))]
    pub display_name: Option<String>,      // 尚無 guest 時由 handler 檢查必填
    #[validate(length(max = 100))]
    pub contact: Option<String>,
    #[validate(length(max = 200))]
    pub note: Option<String>,
    #[validate(email)]
    pub email: Option<String>,             // 選填：確認信與管理連結；存入 guests.email（小寫）
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, sqlx::FromRow, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct ClaimDto {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub item_id: Uuid,
    pub qty: i32,
    pub status: ClaimStatus,
    pub claimer_name: String,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339")] pub created_at: OffsetDateTime,
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339")] pub updated_at: OffsetDateTime,
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339::option")] pub purchased_at: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339::option")] pub delivered_at: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339::option")] pub cancelled_at: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, sqlx::FromRow, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct ItemBrief { pub id: Uuid, pub qty_needed: i32, pub qty_claimed: i32, pub qty_remaining: i32 }

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, utoipa::ToSchema)]
pub struct ClaimResponse {
    pub claim: ClaimDto,
    pub item: ItemBrief,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub guest_token: Option<String>,       // 僅首次建立 guest
}

// ---------- handler 骨架：防超額認領 ----------
pub async fn create_claim(
    State(st): State<AppState>, actor: Actor, Path(item_id): Path<Uuid>,
    IdempotencyKey(key): IdempotencyKey, Json(req): Json<CreateClaimRequest>,
) -> Result<(StatusCode, Json<ClaimResponse>), AppError> {
    req.validate()?;                                           // From<ValidationErrors> for AppError
    let mut tx = st.db.begin().await?;
    if let Some(replay) = idempotency::begin(&mut tx, &key, &actor, &req).await? {
        return Ok(replay);                                     // 同 key 同 body：重放；不同 body：IdempotencyConflict
    }
    let updated = sqlx::query_as!(ItemBrief,
        r#"UPDATE wishlist_items i SET qty_claimed = i.qty_claimed + $2
             FROM wishlists w
            WHERE i.id = $1 AND w.id = i.wishlist_id AND w.status = 'active' AND w.deleted_at IS NULL
              AND i.deleted_at IS NULL AND i.funding_mode = 'quantity'
              AND i.qty_claimed + $2 <= i.qty_needed
        RETURNING i.id, i.qty_needed, i.qty_claimed, (i.qty_needed - i.qty_claimed) AS "qty_remaining!""#,
        item_id, req.qty).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
    let Some(item) = updated else {
        drop(tx);                                              // rollback，再歸因
        return Err(classify_claim_failure(&st.db, item_id).await);   // NotFound / WishlistClosed / ItemFullyClaimed{remaining}
    };
    // INSERT INTO claims ... RETURNING *  → ClaimDto；必要時建立 guest 並產生 guest_token
    // idempotency::finish(&mut tx, &key, 201, &body).await?; tx.commit().await?;
    todo!()
}

// ---------- AppError → application/problem+json ----------
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum AppError {
    #[error("validation failed")]            Validation(Vec<FieldError>),
    #[error("unauthorized")]                 Unauthorized,
    #[error("forbidden")]                    Forbidden,
    #[error("not found")]                    NotFound,
    #[error("rate limited")]                 RateLimited { retry_after_secs: u64 },
    #[error("idempotency conflict")]         IdempotencyConflict,
    #[error("wishlist closed")]              WishlistClosed,
    #[error("item fully claimed")]           ItemFullyClaimed { remaining: i32 },
    #[error("claim already exists")]        ClaimAlreadyExists,
    #[error("insufficient points")]         InsufficientPoints { balance: i64 },
    #[error("crowdfund target exceeded")]   CrowdfundTargetExceeded { remaining_points: i64 },
    #[error("item funded")]                  ItemFunded,
    #[error("funding expired")]             FundingExpired,
    #[error("gift card not yet claimable")] GiftCardNotYetClaimable { claimable_at: OffsetDateTime },
    #[error("topup limit exceeded")]         TopupLimitExceeded { limit_amount: i64, remaining_amount: i64 },
    #[error("wishlist removed")]            WishlistRemoved,
    #[error("account has balance")]         AccountHasBalance,
    #[error("wallet frozen")]                WalletFrozen,
    #[error("org not verified")]             OrgNotVerified,
    #[error("gift card invalid")]            GiftCardInvalid,
    #[error("gift card already claimed")]   GiftCardAlreadyClaimed,
    #[error("gift card not refundable")]    GiftCardNotRefundable,
    #[error("not cash refundable")]          NotCashRefundable { refundable_points: i64 },
    #[error("insufficient stars")]           InsufficientStars,
    #[error("auto top-up limit reached")]    AutoTopupLimitReached,
    #[error("codes already exported")]       CodesAlreadyExported,
    #[error("contribution locked")]          ContributionLocked,
    #[error("payment failed")]               PaymentFailed,
    #[error("staff only")]                   StaffOnly,
    #[error(transparent)]                    Db(#[from] sqlx::Error),
    #[error(transparent)]                    Other(#[from] anyhow::Error),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct FieldError { pub pointer: String, pub code: String, pub detail: String }

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct Problem<'a> {
    r#type: String, title: &'a str, status: u16, detail: String, code: &'a str,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] request_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] errors: Vec<FieldError>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] remaining: Option<i32>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] remaining_points: Option<i64>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] balance: Option<i64>,
}

impl AppError {
    fn parts(&self) -> (StatusCode, &'static str, &'static str) {   // (HTTP, code, title)
        use AppError::*;
        match self {
            Validation(_)          => (StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, "VALIDATION_FAILED", "Validation failed"),
            Unauthorized           => (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Unauthorized"),
            Forbidden              => (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "FORBIDDEN", "Forbidden"),
            NotFound               => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "NOT_FOUND", "Not found"),
            RateLimited { .. }     => (StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, "RATE_LIMITED", "Too many requests"),
            IdempotencyConflict    => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", "Idempotency conflict"),
            WishlistClosed         => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "WISHLIST_CLOSED", "Wishlist closed"),
            ItemFullyClaimed { .. }=> (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED", "Item fully claimed"),
            ClaimAlreadyExists     => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS", "Claim already exists"),
            InsufficientPoints { .. }      => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "INSUFFICIENT_POINTS", "Insufficient points"),
            CrowdfundTargetExceeded { .. } => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED", "Crowdfund target exceeded"),
            ItemFunded             => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "ITEM_FUNDED", "Item already funded"),
            FundingExpired         => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "FUNDING_EXPIRED", "Funding expired"),
            GiftCardNotYetClaimable { .. } => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE", "Gift card not yet claimable"),   // body 附 claimable_at
            TopupLimitExceeded { .. } => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED", "Topup limit exceeded"),                     // body 附 limit_amount、remaining_amount
            WishlistRemoved        => (StatusCode::GONE, "WISHLIST_REMOVED", "Wishlist removed"),          // 公開頁被下架（410）
            AccountHasBalance      => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "ACCOUNT_HAS_BALANCE", "Account has balance"),
            WalletFrozen           => (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "WALLET_FROZEN", "Wallet frozen"),
            OrgNotVerified         => (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "ORG_NOT_VERIFIED", "Organization not verified"),
            GiftCardInvalid        => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "GIFT_CARD_INVALID", "Gift card invalid"),   // 錯誤 / 不存在 / 已退款一律 404，防猜碼
            GiftCardAlreadyClaimed => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "GIFT_CARD_ALREADY_CLAIMED", "Gift card already claimed"),
            GiftCardNotRefundable  => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE", "Gift card not refundable"),
            NotCashRefundable { .. } => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE", "Not cash refundable"),
            InsufficientStars      => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "INSUFFICIENT_STARS", "Insufficient stars"),
            AutoTopupLimitReached  => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED", "Auto top-up limit reached"),
            CodesAlreadyExported   => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED", "Codes already exported"),
            ContributionLocked     => (StatusCode::CONFLICT, "CONTRIBUTION_LOCKED", "Contribution locked"),
            PaymentFailed          => (StatusCode::PAYMENT_REQUIRED, "PAYMENT_FAILED", "Payment failed"),
            StaffOnly              => (StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, "STAFF_ONLY", "Staff only"),
            Db(_) | Other(_)       => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "INTERNAL_ERROR", "Internal server error"),
        }
    }
}

impl IntoResponse for AppError {
    fn into_response(self) -> Response {
        let (status, code, title) = self.parts();
        if matches!(self, AppError::Db(_) | AppError::Other(_)) {
            tracing::error!(error = ?self, "internal error");          // 內部細節只進 log，不回給客戶端
        }
        let body = Problem {
            r#type: format!("https://api.wishsync.tw/problems/{}", code.to_lowercase().replace('_', "-")),
            title, status: status.as_u16(),
            detail: if status.is_server_error() { "發生未預期的錯誤，請稍後再試。".into() } else { self.to_string() },
            code,
            request_id: None,                                          // 由 tower 中介層以 request extension 填入
            errors: if let AppError::Validation(v) = &self { v.clone() } else { vec![] },
            remaining: if let AppError::ItemFullyClaimed { remaining } = &self { Some(*remaining) } else { None },
            remaining_points: if let AppError::CrowdfundTargetExceeded { remaining_points } = &self { Some(*remaining_points) } else { None },
            balance: if let AppError::InsufficientPoints { balance } = &self { Some(*balance) } else { None },
        };
        let mut res = (status, serde_json::to_vec(&body).unwrap()).into_response();
        res.headers_mut().insert(header::CONTENT_TYPE, HeaderValue::from_static("application/problem+json"));
        if let AppError::RateLimited { retry_after_secs } = self {
            res.headers_mut().insert(header::RETRY_AFTER, retry_after_secs.into());
        }
        res
    }
}

// CHECK / UNIQUE 違規的最後防線映射（sqlx::Error 的 SQLSTATE）
impl AppError {
    pub fn from_db(e: sqlx::Error) -> Self {
        if let Some(db) = e.as_database_error() {
            match (db.code().as_deref(), db.constraint()) {
                (Some("23505"), Some("claims_item_guest_active_uk" | "claims_item_user_active_uk"))
                    => return AppError::ClaimAlreadyExists,                           // D6
                (Some("23514"), Some("items_qty_claimed_range"))
                    => return AppError::ItemFullyClaimed { remaining: 0 },            // 最後防線
                (Some("23514"), Some("point_wallets_balance_nonneg"))
                    => return AppError::InsufficientPoints { balance: 0 },            // 最後防線（正常由條件式 UPDATE 擋下）
                (Some("23514"), Some("items_pledged_le_target"))
                    => return AppError::CrowdfundTargetExceeded { remaining_points: 0 }, // 最後防線
                (Some("23514"), _) if db.message() == "ORG_NOT_VERIFIED"
                    => return AppError::OrgNotVerified,                                  // wishlists_check_org_verified trigger
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        AppError::Db(e)
    }
}

8. 錯誤碼總表

「規格」為共用對齊規格已定義；補充 為本文件新增，需同步給 PRD / 後端主流程文件。

codeHTTP說明來源
BAD_REQUEST400JSON 格式錯誤、參數型別錯誤、Content-Type 不符補充
IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED400建立型 POST 缺少 Idempotency-Key 或不是 UUID補充
OAUTH_STATE_INVALID400OAuth state / PKCE 驗證失敗補充
OTP_INVALID400OTP 錯誤 / 過期 / 已使用（不區分）補充
UNAUTHORIZED401未登入、session 過期、guest token 無效規格
PAYMENT_FAILED402儲值扣款失敗（信用卡被拒 / 3D 驗證失敗等）補充
FORBIDDEN403已登入但無權限（非擁有者、組織角色不足、驚喜鎖定期間禁止的操作）規格
ORG_NOT_VERIFIED403未驗證的救災組織不可建立 / 改為 public 據點（D43）補充
STAFF_ONLY403僅營運人員（users.is_staff）可呼叫的 /admin 端點補充
WALLET_FROZEN403錢包因拒付 / 風控被凍結，認捐、兌換（禮物卡 / 星星）、現金退款皆拒絕（D41）補充
GIFT_CARD_INVALID404禮物卡 code 錯誤 / 不存在 / 已退款或作廢；一律 404，不區分原因以防猜碼（D25）補充
NOT_FOUND404資源不存在、已刪除，或無權查看（避免洩漏存在與否）規格
USER_NOT_FOUND404以 email 新增成員時找不到已註冊使用者補充
ACCOUNT_HAS_BALANCE409刪除帳號時錢包餘額 > 0、有未完成認捐（pledged）或未結案訂單；需先處理，不可退現金的點數須明確確認放棄（D40，P2）補充
AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED409自動儲值設定的單次金額大於月上限；月累計超過上限時 job 不扣款，僅以通知提示、不阻擋認捐（D24）補充
CATALOG_PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE409目錄商品已下架、缺貨或商家停用補充
CLAIM_ALREADY_EXISTS409同一人對同一品項已有有效認領（D6）；改數量請用 PATCH /claims/{claim_id}補充
CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED409企業禮券批次的兌換連結 CSV 已匯出過（codes_exported_at 非 NULL），不可再取；遺失請用 reissue 補發（D27 / D31-8）補充
CONTRIBUTION_LOCKED409不可撤回：已 captured（達標後點數已用於購買）、品項已 funded，或 expired 品項已超過 7 天選擇期（D22-1）補充
CROWDFUND_TARGET_EXCEEDED409認捐點數超過目標剩餘額（眾籌一律硬上限，不可超額；附 remaining_points）補充
FUNDING_EXPIRED409對已截止（expired 或已過 funding_deadline）的眾籌品項認捐（D22-2）補充
FUNDING_MODE_MISMATCH409對 crowdfund 品項發起 claim，或對 quantity 品項發起 contribution（認捐）補充
GIFT_CARD_ALREADY_CLAIMED409禮物卡已被兌換（D25）補充
GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE409已兌換的禮物卡不可退款（D25）補充
GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE409高風險訂單的禮物卡尚未到 claimable_at（最多延遲 24 小時才可兌換）；body 附 claimable_at（D41 / D47-1）補充
IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT409同一 Idempotency-Key 但 request body 不同，或前一次請求仍在處理中（附 Retry-After）規格
INSUFFICIENT_POINTS409錢包餘額不足（認捐或現金退款；附 balance）補充
INSUFFICIENT_STARS409星星餘額不足，無法兌換（D26）補充
INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION409claim / purchase_order 狀態機不允許此轉換補充
ITEM_FULLY_CLAIMED409剩餘數量不足（回應附 remaining）規格
ITEM_FUNDED409眾籌品項已達標（funded / fulfilled），不再接受認捐補充
ITEM_HAS_CLAIMS409品項已有進行中認領 / 認捐，不可刪除或變更 funding_mode（除非 force）補充
LAST_OWNER409操作會讓組織失去最後一位 owner補充
MEMBER_ALREADY_EXISTS409該使用者已是組織成員補充
NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE409現金退款金額超過可退現金的點數（僅 topup 批次可退；禮物卡兌入與星星贊助點數不可退現金，D23）補充
QTY_BELOW_CLAIMED409qty_needed 設得比已認領量小補充
REALLOCATION_NOT_ALLOWED409轉投不允許：來源非 pledged / 非 expired 或已過 7 天選擇期、目標品項不在同清單 / 非 open / 超過剩餘補充
SHIPPING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED409含眾籌品項的清單尚未填收件地址，不可發佈或新增眾籌品項補充
SITE_NOT_ACTIVE409relief 據點 site_status 為 paused / closed，不接受認捐或實收登記補充
TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED409新帳號（註冊 30 天內）每日儲值 + 禮物卡購買超過 NT$3,000；涵蓋手動 / 自動儲值與禮物卡，不含營運建立的企業禮券；body 附 limit_amount、remaining_amount（D41 / D47-2）補充
WISHLIST_CLOSED409清單 status 為 closed / archived，或據點 site_status=closed，不再接受認領 / 認捐 / 修改規格
WISHLIST_NOT_PUBLISHABLE409不符發佈條件（無品項、title 空白、眾籌品項缺 target_points / funding_deadline 等；errors[] 列出缺漏）補充
WISHLIST_REMOVED410清單已被下架（moderation_status = hidden）；公開頁 / SSE / 檢舉端點皆回 410，body 不含下架原因（D35）補充
URL_BLOCKED422link-preview 目標被 SSRF 防護拒絕（P1）補充
VALIDATION_FAILED422欄位驗證失敗（errors[] 列出每個欄位）規格
RATE_LIMITED429超過限流（附 Retry-After 與 RateLimit-* header）規格
INTERNAL_ERROR500未預期的伺服器錯誤（細節只進 log）補充
OAUTH_PROVIDER_ERROR502LINE / Google 端點錯誤或逾時（OAuth token 交換失敗）補充
READ_ONLY_MODE503系統處於唯讀模式（system_flags.read_only），非安全方法被拒絕（附 Retry-After）補充
SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE503DB 連線池耗盡 / 維護中（附 Retry-After）補充
UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT504link-preview 抓取逾時（P1）補充

9. 版本策略與相容性

  • URL 版本：主版號放在路徑 /api/v1。破壞性變更才升 /api/v2，v1 至少保留 6 個月並以 Deprecation / Sunset header 預告。
  • 欄位只增不刪：v1 內不得移除或改名既有欄位、不得改變型別 / 單位 / 枚舉語意；只可新增可選欄位、新增枚舉值。前端 TypeScript 型別對 enum 需容忍未知值（fallback 顯示）。
  • 請求端寬鬆：伺服器忽略未知的 request 欄位；新增必填欄位視為破壞性變更。
  • 新增錯誤碼：視為相容變更，前端須有 default 錯誤處理。
  • OpenAPI 3.1：建議以 utoipa（搭配 utoipa-axum）由 Rust handler / DTO 產生 openapi.json，CI 中以 oasdiff 比對偵測破壞性變更，並用來產生前端型別與 API 文件。本文件為人工契約基準，之後以 OpenAPI 為單一事實來源。
  • 資料庫遷移：sqlx migrate；採 expand/contract（先加欄位、雙寫、再移除），確保滾動部署期間新舊版本共存。
  • SSE 事件：事件名稱與既有欄位同樣只增不刪。

10. 待決問題

  1. 法規：禮物卡兌換後不可退現金（D30）：禮物卡兌換後點數「不可退現金、只能用於心願品項」的條款，需律師確認是否符合禮券定型化契約規範；如不符，退款對象改為原購買者（需變更 gift_card 批次的 cash_refundable 與兌換退款流程）。履約保證範圍（儲值點數 + 未兌換禮物卡 + 已兌換未使用的 gift_card 點數，reward_bonus 除外）的細節與機構同樣由律師確認。
  2. 法規與會計：發票開立時點：儲值、禮物卡、企業禮券（三聯式含統編）的發票開立時點（儲值 / 購買時或兌換使用時），需會計師確認；Schema 已改以 invoices 表關聯（topups.invoice_id、gift_card_orders.invoice_id、gift_card_batches.invoice_id），兩種時點皆可支援。星星與贊助點數為無償贈與、可設期限，須載明於使用條款。
  3. 過期批次的作廢記帳：已過期 reward_bonus 批次的退點，本文件在 ledger 記一筆 release 並立即以一筆 expire 抵銷（因 delta 不得為 0），wallet 不增加。
  4. 過期 bonus 在餘額顯示上的空窗：D31-7 已確定扣點只取未過期批次；但每日 job 之間，已過期的 bonus 仍暫計入 wallet.balance 與 GET /wallet 的 balance（bonus_points 已排除）。是否改為小時級 job，或讀取餘額時即時扣除？
  5. 星星到期的消耗順序：星星到期假設先進先出（先被兌換 / 扣回的視為消耗最舊的星星）；是否需要逐批追蹤剩餘（星星批次表）？
  6. 星星賺取基準：D31-5 已定：給星點數 = captured 認捐中來自 gift_card + topup 批次的 allocations 加總，每 50 點 1 星（無條件捨去，按每筆認捐計算）。小額多筆認捐會損失零頭，是否改為以品項達標後的總額計算？
  7. target_points 計算公式：D22-8 給了 concierge_fee_rate 0.08 與 concierge_buffer_rate 0.10，未定義組合方式。本文件暫定 target_points = 價格（含運）+ ceil(價格 × fee_rate) + ceil(價格 × buffer_rate)；目錄商品的緩衝比例與向商家收分潤的記帳位置（schema 目前沒有分潤欄位）仍待營運決定。
  8. 現金退款路徑：現金退款是原路退回還是匯款到指定帳戶（cash_refund_requests.destination_enc 已預留）、手續費由誰負擔，待定。
  9. 商家 webhook 與商家 API：出貨 / 送達由商家 webhook 或 staff 更新：商家 webhook 端點、驗簽方式與下單 API 要等合作商家串接規格確定後補入。
  10. point_ledger 唯一索引範圍：D22-4 要求 UNIQUE(wallet_id, entry_type, ref_type, ref_id)。本文件做成「部分」唯一並加入 lot_id（僅 topup / contribution / cash_refund_request / gift_card 來源），因為購買失敗重試時 purchase_order 來源的 release 合法地會重複，且一次認捐可跨多批次。請確認。
  11. 訪客 guest 合併到 user：D47-3 已定訪客恢復機制（一次性恢復權杖）。訪客後來註冊 / 登入時，其 guest 的認領是否合併到 user 仍待定；目前 claims 可有 guest_id 或 user_id 其一，不處理合併。
  12. 金融與個資的保存年限：帳號刪除後 ledger、topups、invoices、purchase_orders 依商業會計法保留並假名化（D40），年限待會計師確認；guests.contact / 收件資訊 / audit_logs 的保存期限同需與 PRD 隱私條款對齊。
  13. 組織邀請與移除成員：organizations 的驗證流程已定（D43）；邀請成員目前要求對方已註冊（否則 USER_NOT_FOUND），尚無 email 邀請連結流程；也未提供移除成員 API。
  14. 座標順序：規格中 bbox 為 minLng,minLat,maxLng,maxLat，near 卻是 lat,lng。本文件照規格實作並在 location 物件使用 {lat, lng} 具名欄位，避免順序混淆。
  15. link-preview 圖片轉存：抓到的商品圖是直接 hotlink 還是由後端轉存 R2？轉存較穩定，但需處理版權與 SSRF 面積。
  16. SSE 在 Cloud Run 的成本：Cloud Run 單請求逾時上限與連線計費對長連線不友善。建議 MVP 評估 Fly.io，或把 SSE 逾時設 5 分鐘讓 EventSource 自動重連，並預留 polling fallback。
  17. 活動日時區：unlock_at 與 funding_deadline 以 Asia/Taipei 計算；海外使用者（跨時區）是否需要 per-wishlist 時區欄位？
  18. relief 與點數：relief 仍只用數量型認領（claims + receipts）。未來可讓捐贈者用點數購買目錄物資直送據點，本版不處理。

已決（審查裁決，僅供對照）

  • D47 補充裁決：①GIFT_CARD_NOT_YET_CLAIMABLE 409（附 claimable_at）取代暫用的 INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION；②TOPUP_LIMIT_EXCEEDED 409（附 limit_amount、remaining_amount），新帳號每日 NT$3,000 涵蓋手動儲值、自動儲值、禮物卡購買，不含營運建立的企業禮券，超限直接拒絕，異常行為另由風控進人工審核；③訪客管理連結改為一次性恢復權杖（guest_recovery_tokens、POST /guest/recover，信件不放 guest token，無效 / 過期 / 已用一律 404），guest_recovery_tokens 納入 MVP 遷移；④GET /unsubscribe?token=（HMAC 簽章，免登入一鍵退訂）；⑤notification_prefs 鍵名 email_claims（不使用 notify_email），用 PATCH /me 更新；⑥wishlist_items.image_status（none | pending | ready | rejected），處理完成前公開頁不顯示圖片；⑦儲值與禮物卡訂單 request 用巢狀 invoice 物件；⑧PATCH /admin/wallets/{id}、GET /admin/wallet-debts、PATCH /admin/wallet-debts/{id}（P2-A，STAFF_ONLY）。
  • v0.4 補齊遺漏：D35 檢舉與下架（content_reports、moderation_*、hidden → 410 WISHLIST_REMOVED、圖片去 EXIF）；D36 MVP 營運後台（users.is_staff 為 MVP、/admin/* STAFF_ONLY、動作寫 audit_logs actor_type=staff）；D39 MVP Email 通知（notifications 為 MVP outbox，第一筆即時、之後每日彙整，驚喜模式只寫件數）；D40 帳號刪除與資料匯出（DELETE /me、GET /me/export、DELETE /guest/me）；D41 信用卡拒付（3DS、錢包凍結、先扣回 topup 批次、不足記 wallet_debts、禮物卡延遲兌換）；D42 電子發票（invoices，topups / gift_card_orders / gift_card_batches 改關聯）；D43 救災組織驗證（verification_status、ORG_NOT_VERIFIED）；D46 新錯誤碼 WISHLIST_REMOVED、ACCOUNT_HAS_BALANCE、WALLET_FROZEN、ORG_NOT_VERIFIED。
  • 通知設定的 API 形式：採 PATCH /me 的 notification_prefs 欄位（不另開端點），GET /me 一併回傳。
  • MVP 遷移範圍：M1 只建立 14 張表（users、auth_identities、sessions、otp_challenges、guests、guest_recovery_tokens、wishlists、wishlist_items、claims、idempotency_keys、audit_logs、system_flags、notifications、content_reports），其餘依 P2-A 到 P2-D 分批遷移。
  • v0.3 效仿星巴克：維持點數錢包（知悉禮券法規負擔，視為必要成本），新增：隨行卡式自動儲值、電子禮物卡、回饋星星、企業禮券。核心原則不變：點數永遠不能提領成現金給受捐者，只能用在心願品項。
  • P2 細分：P2-A 儲值錢包 + 點數眾籌 + 目錄 / 代購履約 + 自動儲值；P2-B 電子禮物卡 + 回饋星星；P2-C 企業禮券（初期營運後台代建）；P2-D relief 救災。徽章仍為 P2，文字標子階段。
  • D23 點數批次：topup（可退現金、不到期）/ gift_card（不可退現金、不到期）/ reward_bonus（不可退現金、90 天到期、非預收款）；扣點 bonus（最早到期）→ gift → topup；released 退回原批次，過期 bonus 作廢；現金退款只退 cash_refundable，否則 NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE；ledger 加 lot_id 與 gift_claim / reward_bonus / expire；每日 job 到期 bonus。
  • D24 自動儲值：認捐後餘額低於門檻 → outbox 非同步扣款；月上限預設 10,000，超過不扣並通知（AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED 僅出現在設定 / 通知）；認捐餘額不足仍回 INSUFFICIENT_POINTS。
  • D25 電子禮物卡：面額 300 / 500 / 1000 / 2000 / 3000，一次 1–10 張；code 16 碼 Crockford base32，DB 只存 hash；兌換需登入 + Turnstile + 限流；未兌換可退款，已兌換 → GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE；售出款納入履約保證。
  • D26 回饋星星：captured 認捐每 50 點 1 星（儲值與禮物卡購買不給）；失敗扣回、不為負；gold = 12 個月內 ≥ 300 星；green 100 星 = 50 點、gold 100 星 = 60 點；星星 12 個月到期。
  • D27 企業禮券：corporate_accounts + gift_card_batches（draft → invoiced → paid → issued / void）；營運後台操作；CSV 只能匯出一次。
  • D31 補充裁決：①code_enc 清除時機：個人卡 claimed / refunded / void 時清為 NULL（購買者可隨時重看連結），企業卡匯出後清除；②購買流程：POST /gift-cards/orders 回 order 與金流參數，付款後輪詢 GET /gift-cards/orders/{id}（paid 後回 cards[].share_url），GET /gift-cards/purchased 為列表；③GET /config/public 加 gift_card_face_values、gift_card_max_quantity、gift_card_themes，祝福語以訂單為單位；④ledger 唯一鍵含 lot_id；⑤以 reward_bonus 批次支付的點數不給星，給星點數 = gift_card + topup 批次 allocations 加總；⑥部分退點逆序 topup → gift_card → reward_bonus，整筆 released 全數退回原批次；⑦扣點只取 expires_at IS NULL 或未到期的批次；⑧企業 CSV 遺失：POST /admin/gift-card-batches/{id}/reissue，codes_exported_at 重置。
  • D29 / D22 錯誤碼：新增 GIFT_CARD_INVALID、GIFT_CARD_ALREADY_CLAIMED、GIFT_CARD_NOT_REFUNDABLE、NOT_CASH_REFUNDABLE、INSUFFICIENT_STARS、AUTO_TOPUP_LIMIT_REACHED、CODES_ALREADY_EXPORTED。
  • D22 補充裁決：撤回規則（open，或 expired 且 now < expired_at + 7d）、FUNDING_EXPIRED、GET /wallet/contributions、補充欄位、*_amount / *_points 命名、display_status、topups method、GET /config/public、admin orders / cash-refunds、驚喜模式通知與彙總、鎖順序 wishlist → wallet → item、pledged_points 定義、公開頁 id / owner.id / surprise_mode。
  • v0.2 眾籌：眾籌改為點數錢包 + 達標自動購買，移到 P2；MVP 只做數量型認領。眾籌一律硬上限。
  • D4：驚喜模式：unlock_at = event_date 當日 00:00 Asia/Taipei；鎖定期間建立者端只回整體彙總，公開頁仍顯示 x/y，由前端對建立者本人做軟性遮罩（已知限制）。眾籌捐贈者 is_anonymous=true 時連解鎖後也只顯示「匿名朋友」。
  • D5–D13：delivered 標記權限、同一人僅一筆有效認領、claim_ttl_hours、背景工作 tick 與唯讀模式、receipts.client_request_id、relief 沿用 claims、據點頁用 slug、發佈用 PATCH status=active、DELETE 清單＝封存。
  • 履約：目錄商品平台自動下單、非目錄商品平台人工代購；截止未達標由捐贈者選擇，預設退回錢包。
